Relevant materials include contracts, agreements, notices, letters, emails, messages, screenshots, forms, court papers, government or agency communications, insurance materials, existing legal documents, and other portal-appropriate records.

10. SUGGESTED NEXT STEPS

Every completed document or screenshot review must provide prioritized, practical, plain-language suggested next steps, while avoiding legal conclusions, guaranteed deadlines, guaranteed outcomes, unsupported instructions to sign or file, and false claims of attorney review.

11. USER WORKSPACE AND CONTINUITY

Where appropriate, public users should be able to save, resume, organize, export, delete, and understand the status of their forms, drafts, uploads, reviews, timelines, evidence, and attorney-review requests through a privacy-minimized workspace.

12. CONFLICT AND CONFIDENTIALITY CONTROL

Before confidential material is released to an attorney, the system must support an appropriate preliminary conflict, eligibility, consent, and attorney-acceptance step. A request or allocation does not create an attorney-client relationship.

13. CLEAR STATUS, SUPPORT, AND RECOVERY

Users and professionals must receive truthful status, understandable errors, recovery paths, support, and escalation. Silent failure, fabricated completion, dead controls, and misleading “submitted,” “reviewed,” “assigned,” “filed,” or “accepted” states are prohibited.

14. ACCESSIBILITY, MOBILE, PRIVACY, SECURITY, AND EXACT-ARTIFACT QUALITY

All capabilities remain subject to the complete accessibility, responsive-design, privacy, security, source-rights, legal-compliance, monitoring, support, rollback, testing, and exact-packaged-artifact requirements in this instruction.


14A. SURVIVOR-SAFETY OVERLAY FOR DOMESTIC VIOLENCE AID

Domestic Violence Aid must apply a survivor-centered safety overlay to public pages, forms, search, profiles, analytics, communication, uploads, workspaces, exports, logs, support, and cross-portal handoffs. Safety requirements include truthful emergency boundaries, safe-device guidance, quick-exit limitations, discreet page and notification behavior, minimal data collection, deliberate rather than silent saving, no confidential-location exposure, safe-contact preferences, privacy-safe URLs and referrers, and explicit consent before sharing sensitive information.

14B. STOP SIGN PROJECT PRESERVATION AND EMBEDDING

The Stop Sign Project remains a working community initiative within Domestic Violence Aid. Rebranding must not remove, bury, disable, or misrepresent it. Its routes, public identity, tools, educational and outreach functions, profiles, source-tracking, correction rights, and direct doorway must be preserved and tested unless a documented material reason supports a safer revision.

15. ATTORNEY-OUTREACH FIRST-IMPRESSION AND DEMONSTRATION READINESS

Each portal must be visually and operationally credible enough for Roger to show
it during an unplanned or short law-office visit. The homepage, attorney search,
one exemplary canonical profile, one strong public tool, the professional-value
path, and the mobile continuation path must be polished, coherent, safe, and
free of broken or visibly unfinished states.

16. QUALIFIED PROFESSIONAL-MEMBERSHIP AND JUSTICE BOOTH PRESENTATION

Each portal must provide truthful professional-facing context that links to the
current Smarter Justice membership and central management flow. Comparative
cost or retained-matter value language must be qualified, owner approved, and
must never promise a lead, client, revenue, case value, ranking, exclusivity, or
return on investment. New York City Justice Booth statements must distinguish
PLANNED, SCHEDULED, ACTIVE, COMPLETED, and VERIFIED LOCATION status.

17. COHERENT DOCUMENT-HELP CENTER

Each portal must have or progressively build one clear Document Help entry area
that routes users into separate specialty-appropriate workflows instead of
forcing unrelated document functions into one confusing tool. Official forms,
portal-created preparation materials, document review, drafting, attorney
review, and electronic filing must remain visibly distinct.

These requirements are cumulative. A later build must not omit or weaken one merely because another capability becomes the immediate release focus.

QUICK OPERATING SUMMARY FOR EVERY MODE A PORTAL REUSE
===============================================================================

This is a standing build instruction, not a historical snapshot and not a one-time checklist.

For every use:

1. Work in exactly one dedicated micro-portal repository.
2. Locate and verify the newest completed exact portal ZIP in that portal’s chat.
3. Locate and verify the newest applicable exact Smarter Justice handoff available to that chat.
4. Preserve an untouched baseline and all verified working functionality.
5. Classify the release type and perform the corresponding portal-wide scan,
   affected-surface deep audit, or full milestone audit.
6. Select a coherent material release scope based on evidence.
7. For every ordinary material release, add a meaningful lawful batch of new,
   high-quality, specialty-qualified accepted professional profiles and refresh
   or enrich existing accepted profiles. Both outcomes are mandatory whether or
   not the release's headline feature concerns profiles.
8. Use a Profile-Growth Exception Receipt only for a narrowly classified non-
   ordinary emergency, rollback, recovery, artifact-integrity correction, or
   genuine no-change outcome. Lack of time, competing work, difficulty, release
   size, or failure to search is not an exception. Preserve Domestic Violence
   Aid organization/service and Stop Sign Project relationship safeguards where
   applicable.
9. Ensure accepted public profiles become complete canonical searchable pages.
10. Verify every attorney, firm representative, organization representative, or other authorized claimant for the exact profile and authority type before granting control.
11. Keep basic claiming, verification, correction, and approved editing free.
12. Keep source-based publication, claim outreach, claimant verification, and profile control as separate auditable processes.
13. Keep complete public profile pages on the micro-portal and central professional management in Smarter Justice.
14. Proactively improve public UX, branding, content, tools, conversion, operations, accessibility, security, privacy, reliability, data quality, integration, testing, and release quality where evidence supports it; do not wait for Roger to identify the opportunity.
15. Review relevant accepted practices from Smarter Justice and the other portals; adopt or adapt them when suitable and record a specific reason for material divergence.
16. Audit and, where materially needed, improve the attorney-outreach demonstration path: homepage, search, one canonical profile, one strong tool, professional value, and QR or short-link continuation.
17. Implement or refine the portal's coherent Document Help entry area and separate specialty-appropriate workflows without falsely presenting unfinished capabilities as live.
18. Keep professional membership, comparative cost, retained-matter value,
    Justice Booth, and outreach statements current, qualified, owner-approved,
    and evidence-classified. Mention Justice Truck only when the exact portal
    already uses that separate brand and current owner-approved evidence
    supports the specific statement.
19. Apply the English-first standard: complete and exact-test English, preserve dormant language assets, and keep Spanish and additional languages inactive unless separately approved.
20. Test the exact final packaged artifact after independent fresh extractions.
21. Update current truth, unresolved work, profile metrics, integration maturity, rollback, and the reusable continuation prompt.
22. Deliver one clean next material release ZIP when safe material work can be completed and a release is materially justified.
23. If no material release is justified, preserve the exact base and provide an honest no-change audit result instead of manufacturing a version.
24. Do not deploy or open a gated live feature without the required exact evidence and Roger’s explicit approval.

The builder must not respond with only a plan when safe material implementation can be completed in the current build.

   universal portal registry, applicable initial-launch five-platform register,

   coordination records.

  review;
- exact owner, qualified-reviewer, vendor, legal, accessibility, hosting, DNS,
  payment, email, database, storage, monitoring, AI/OCR, and production actions;
- current fallback scope and overdue or superseded target dates; and
- evidence classification for every fact.

No Mode B2 code change may begin until the exact Smarter Justice source and
central authority are clear. Mode B1 may reconcile records immediately but may
not edit a product repository.

OWNER INTENT, IMPLEMENTATION TRUTH, CROSS-CHAT EVIDENCE, AND CONFLICT RESOLUTION
===============================================================================

Do not use one blended hierarchy for both desired future work and present
implementation truth.

OWNER INTENT AND BUILD AUTHORITY

Apply intended requirements in this order:

1. Roger's newest explicit owner decision in the current conversation.
2. The newest complete product-specific mission for the active mode: portal-
   specific continuation/build mission in Mode A, or Smarter Justice central/
   coordination continuation in Mode B.
3. Newer explicit owner decisions that apply to the same subject.
4. This reusable master instruction.
5. The newest applicable Smarter Justice contract, coordination handoff, or
   approved shared standard for the relevant boundary.
6. Older prompts, summaries, plans, roadmaps, and historical release records.
7. Recommendations, assumptions, memories, and inferred preferences.

IMPLEMENTATION AND FACTUAL TRUTH

MODE A

1. The newest exact completed authoritative assigned-portal artifact.
2. Actual current-chat inspection and independently reproduced behavior from
   that portal artifact.
3. The newest exact Smarter Justice contract or coordination artifact that both
   systems can support safely.
4. Current authoritative official external sources where public facts, law,
   forms, agencies, professional status, or official resources matter.
5. Current portal test, staging, deployment, infrastructure, and operational
   evidence.
6. Current artifact documentation that agrees with inspected implementation.
7. Owner-recorded exact identity or context not independently reproduced.
8. Historical descriptions, older artifacts, plans, screenshots, memories,
   fixtures, mocks, and prompt claims.

MODE B

1. The newest exact completed authoritative Smarter Justice artifact and the
   current canonical coordination records for the relevant central fact.
2. Actual current-chat inspection and independently reproduced Smarter Justice
   behavior or exact coordination receipts.
3. Each affected portal's newest exact artifact or current exact handoff, only
   for that portal's product-specific fact and only to the depth material to the
   coordination decision.
4. Current authoritative official external sources.
5. Current D4/D5, deployment, infrastructure, vendor, support, monitoring, and
   operational evidence.
6. Current documentation that agrees with exact implementation and receipts.
7. Owner-recorded context not independently reproduced.
8. Historical descriptions, plans, screenshots, fixtures, mocks, and prompt
   claims.

A portal artifact cannot prove a Smarter Justice central implementation, and a
Smarter Justice artifact cannot prove a portal consumer or product acceptance.
A canonical coordination row records truth; it does not create the underlying
product truth. A newer artifact does not automatically supersede a later owner
decision that has not yet been implemented. A source prompt may control desired
future work while containing stale implementation claims. A higher version
number does not prove completion, authority, safety, or lineage.

CROSS-CHAT EVIDENCE AND REPRODUCTION LANGUAGE

A fresh builder chat does not inherit the prior chat's active filesystem, tool
state, extraction directories, browser session, terminal state, or
reproducibility evidence merely because a continuation prompt records the
result.

Use these evidence states where relevant:

- PRIOR-CHAT VERIFIED RECORD: a prior chat reports exact inspection or
  reproduction and preserves traceable evidence;
- CURRENT-CHAT OWNER-RECORDED: Roger supplies or confirms the fact, but the
  current builder has not independently reproduced it;
- CURRENT-CHAT EXACTLY INSPECTED: the current builder has the exact artifact
  and independently verifies the fact;
- CURRENT-CHAT REPRODUCED: the current builder reruns the relevant test, audit,
  extraction, browser journey, or calculation;
- STALE;
- SUPERSEDED;
- UNKNOWN; and
- NOT NEEDED FOR THIS BUILD.

Do not say `I independently verified`, `I reproduced`, `I inspected`,
`browser-verified`, `staging-accepted`, or equivalent current-session language
based only on a prior handoff, owner memory, a copied summary, a screenshot, or
another builder's statement.

A prompt is instruction evidence, not implementation evidence. A learning
packet is lineage evidence, not automatic proof that the source or target still
works in the newest exact artifact.

Re-inspect and reproduce when the current build materially depends on exact
identity, counts, routes, behavior, currentness, safety, security,
authorization, staging, deployment, release acceptance, rollback, or a
cross-platform learning claim. Do not repeat a deep historical audit when
current prompt-governance context is sufficient and implementation-level truth
is not material.

When a conflict cannot be reconciled safely:

- record the conflict and the evidence states;
- preserve the exact baseline;
- keep the affected feature closed;
- complete all independent safe work;
- do not silently choose the source that is easiest to implement; and
- escalate only the genuine owner, legal, credential, deployment,
  irreversible-risk, or incompatible-authority decision.

NO-LOSS REQUIREMENT TRACEABILITY AND PROMPT-REGRESSION CONTROL
===============================================================================

Every material controlling requirement must be accounted for across:

- Roger's newest explicit owner decisions;
- the newest complete portal-specific continuation or build mission;
- this reusable master instruction;
- any stricter portal-specific safety, privacy, legal, accessibility,
  source-rights, authorization, or rollback standard;
- the exact authoritative artifact's working behavior and release obligations;
- the current supported Smarter Justice boundary; and
- accepted current learning that the target is required to adopt or adapt.

inside the Release Evidence Index rather than created as a duplicative file.
Use dispositions such as:

- PRESERVED;
- IMPLEMENTED;
- IMPROVED;
- CLARIFIED;
- REORGANIZED WITHOUT LOSS;
- REVALIDATED AGAINST THE NEWEST ARTIFACT;
- ADAPTED FOR THIS PORTAL;
- KEPT PUBLICLY GATED;
- CORRECTED AS STALE;
- SUPERSEDED BY A NEWER OWNER DECISION;
- DEFERRED WITH A GENUINE DEPENDENCY;
- BLOCKED WITH A CLOSED GATE;
- REJECTED WITH EVIDENCE;
- NOT APPLICABLE WITH EVIDENCE; or
- OWNER DECISION REQUIRED.

Do not silently:

- omit a material requirement;
- weaken `must` into `should`;
- change a prohibition into a preference;
- remove a gate, test, deliverable, rollback control, correction right, or
  safety safeguard;
- convert current work into future work merely to reduce scope;
- convert deferred work into a public promise;
- let a reusable standard erase a stricter portal-specific requirement; or
- let a stale portal handoff override the newest exact implementation truth.

Before final packaging, perform a prompt-regression check against every
controlling input. Confirm that each material requirement has an explicit
status and that the implemented release, closed gates, evidence, continuation
handoff, and Next Version Improvement List agree. Consolidation is allowed;
loss is not.

===============================================================================
REAUDIT, REFINEMENT, AND INTERPRETATION STATUS
===============================================================================

This universal descendant preserves the complete intent and historical lineage of the prior four-portal build prompt and its Domestic Violence Aid expansion, while strengthening the areas most likely to make every assigned portal useful, differentiated, scalable, safe, maintainable, and truthfully releasable; launchability remains a separate overlay for the initial cohort:

- Builder-originated product discovery and innovation without waiting for Roger to specify every improvement.
- Full-platform work across public experience, backend, data, professional profiles, operations, security, accessibility, reliability, growth, and exact-release quality.
- A faster lawful profile-growth system based on official identity sources, firm-first expansion, specialty evidence, batch operations, provenance, currentness, correction rights, and professional self-service.
- Precise separation between Smarter Justice central authority and each portal's local public rendering and specialty authority.
- A mandatory cooperative-learning loop so every verified improvement can be evaluated by Smarter Justice and the other micro-portals.
- Stronger evidence classifications, stop conditions, release gates, and definitions of done.

Interpretation rules:

- “Must” and “must not” are mandatory.
- “Should” is expected unless exact evidence supports a safer or more effective approach.
- “May” is optional and must not silently become launch scope.
- “Implemented” means behavior exists in the exact inspected source.
- “Verified” means the behavior was reproduced with appropriate tests or exact-artifact inspection.
- “Browser verified,” “staging accepted,” “production active,” and “legally approved” require their own authentic evidence.
- A route, schema, fixture, mock, sample export, screenshot, policy file, or passing unit test does not prove production operation.
- Historical records remain evidence of history; they do not override the newest explicit owner decision.
- When instructions conflict, use the newest explicit owner decision and the stricter truth-, privacy-, security-, accessibility-, rollback-, legal-compliance-, or evidence-preserving interpretation. Record the conflict rather than silently choosing convenience.

This is one mode-specializing prompt. In Mode A, apply all shared requirements
and only the portal-specific subsection for the assigned portal. In Mode B,
apply shared governance and Smarter Justice central/coordination requirements;
treat portal-specific sections as read-only product constraints.

===============================================================================
ALL-LEGAL-MICRO-PORTAL APPLICABILITY AND INITIAL-LAUNCH COHORT MATURITY MATRIX — MODE A
===============================================================================

At the beginning of every build, classify each major capability for the assigned portal as:

- REQUIRED NOW
- REQUIRED TO MATURE
- REQUIRED BUT PUBLICLY GATED
- REQUIRED WHERE APPROPRIATE
- PORTAL-SPECIFIC
- DEFERRED WITH EVIDENCE
- NOT APPLICABLE WITH EVIDENCE
- BLOCKED BY AUTHENTIC EXTERNAL DEPENDENCY

Do not force identical functionality where the legal specialty or survivor-safety context makes a shared pattern inappropriate.

Examples:

- Attorney and firm canonical pages apply to every registered portal that publishes those profile classes.
- Approved organization/service-provider pages are mandatory for Domestic Violence Aid when the portal publishes those profile classes and optional elsewhere when supported.
- Paid-member legal-opportunity allocation applies only to verified attorneys or covered firm seats, never to shelters, nonprofits, agencies, advocates, or emergency services unless a separately lawful non-legal program is explicitly designed and approved.
- Estate will/trust drafts apply to Estate Law Aid.
- Prenuptial/postnuptial drafts apply to Divorce Law Aid.
- Personal-injury evidence and insurance preparation applies to Personal Injury Law Aid.
- Domestic Violence Aid should prioritize safety-oriented organizers, evidence indexes, court/agency preparation, safe-contact choices, resource navigation, and attorney-preparation materials. It must not make public launch depend on risky automated legal documents or unsafe confidential-data collection.
- Document upload and workspace capabilities are especially sensitive in Domestic Violence Aid and remain fail closed until safe-device, storage, retention, deletion, access, incident, support, and legal/privacy acceptance are complete.
- The Stop Sign Project preservation and embedding requirements apply only to Domestic Violence Aid.

Record the classification and reason. “Not applicable” must not be used as a shortcut to avoid necessary work.

NONNEGOTIABLE PORTAL OUTCOMES — MODE A
===============================================================================

The next material release must make the assigned portal measurably stronger as
a complete product. The builder must evaluate every outcome below, preserve all
applicable standing requirements, and select the highest-value connected subset
that can be completed and tested coherently now. The list is not an instruction
to implement every incomplete capability in one release. Any applicable P0
safety, privacy, security, authorization, data-integrity, artifact-truth, or
public-misrepresentation defect must be addressed or kept safely gated:

1. A clearer, more useful, more trustworthy public legal starting experience that makes a credible first impression within seconds.
2. A concise attorney-demonstration path that can show the portal homepage, one exemplary canonical profile, one strong public tool, professional value, and a direct QR or short-link continuation without exposing private data or pretending a gated feature is live.
3. Exceptional canonical attorney and firm pages that are searchable, claimable, correctable, mobile-ready, accessible, and truthfully sourced, plus complete approved organization/service-provider pages where the assigned portal supports those profile classes.
4. A repeatable lawful profile-growth operation that can add and refresh many appropriate profiles faster without sacrificing specialty or service relevance, provenance, safe-contact rules, correction rights, confidential-location suppression, or review quality.
5. Safe central claim, management, verification, firm, billing, and entitlement coordination through Smarter Justice, while the portal retains local specialty content, search, public URLs, and public rendering.
6. Better owner and staff controls so routine profile, source, correction, relationship, import, support, and currentness work does not require developer edits.
7. Stronger backend integrity, security, privacy, accessibility, performance, resilience, observability, testing, and exact-artifact release quality.
8. Document builder-originated opportunities only where the audit identifies a
material problem, missed opportunity, or reusable lesson. Select the
highest-value connected feasible opportunities for implementation and preserve,
defer, reject, or mark the remainder not applicable. Do not manufacture an idea
for every product area merely to satisfy a documentation quota.
8A. Maintain a Best-Practice Adoption and Divergence Record showing which relevant accepted practices from Smarter Justice and the other portals were adopted, adapted, verified, deferred, rejected, or found not applicable, with a specific evidence-based reason for every material divergence.
9. A reusable Cross-Portal Learning Packet that allows Smarter Justice and other registered portals to evaluate what should be adopted, adapted, verified, deferred, or rejected.
10. A verified-claim system in which every claimant is authenticated, identity-checked, and authorized for the exact profile and authority type before control is granted; professional-status verification remains mandatory for attorneys, and organization-authority verification remains mandatory for organization/service profiles.
11. A controlled publication and outreach system that may scale lawful profile discovery and claim invitations without bypassing human review, legal compliance, privacy, suppression, deliverability, or owner gates.
12. One unique, full, canonical public profile page for every approved attorney and every approved firm, and for every approved Domestic Violence Aid organization/service profile that is authorized for publication. Search-result cards, generic shared pages, modal-only listings, central management pages, or incomplete directory records do not substitute for complete entity-specific profile pages.
13. A real, auditable attorney-review and legal-help opportunity-allocation system that delivers opportunities only to verified, portal-eligible attorneys or covered firm seats with an active paid monthly profile membership before receiving any referral or attorney-review opportunity from the network.
14. Continuous evidence-based tool governance through a current inventory,
portal-wide regression/truth scans, and deep review of changed, affected,
launch-critical, stale, high-risk, or selected tools, with useful improvements
implemented when reasonably possible and working tools preserved when change
would not produce a genuine benefit.
15. A reusable guided-forms system through which public users can complete appropriate basic forms, organizers, questionnaires, letters, checklists, intake materials, and preparation records.
16. Jurisdiction-controlled draft document creation, including supported wills and trusts for Estate Law Aid and supported prenuptial and postnuptial agreement drafts for Divorce Law Aid, with strict legal-review, draft-status, execution, privacy, and public-activation gates.
17. A secure document and screenshot review center in every portal where appropriate, supporting private upload, organization, extraction, comparison, analysis, issue identification, review-package creation, attorney-review handoff, and safe suggested next steps.
18. A coherent Document Help Center that clearly separates document review, comparison, drafting, official-form discovery, guided form preparation, attorney-review preparation, and any advanced form-builder access according to actual maturity.
19. Truthful professional-facing membership and New York City outreach presentation that uses current owner-approved pricing, qualified value language, and verified Justice Booth status without promising commercial results.

The builder is accountable for discovering worthwhile work. Roger is not required to identify each defect, feature, workflow, source, design improvement, backend improvement, or growth opportunity.

2. CURRENT SMARTER JUSTICE COORDINATION BASE — DISCOVER, DO NOT ASSUME
===============================================================================

MODE APPLICATION

- In Mode A, this section defines the portal's dependency on and local boundary
  with Smarter Justice.
- In Mode B, the exact Smarter Justice artifact is the primary central
  implementation source, and portal records are read-only consumer evidence.

Smarter Justice is the central professional account, identity, claim, verification, shared profile-management, firm, office, seat, membership, billing, entitlement, support, approved handoff, and owner-operations system for the legal micro-portal network.

The assigned portal remains the public specialty product and owns:

- Specialty brand and domain
- Public legal content
- Specialty starting paths and tools
- Public attorney and firm directories
- Approved organization/service-provider directories where the portal supports them
- Canonical attorney and firm pages
- Canonical organization/service-provider pages where applicable
- Local specialty evidence
- Local public search
- Local public URLs
- Local rendering
- Local import state
- Last-known-good projection
- Local suppression
- Portal deployment and rollback

DO NOT ASSUME A SMARTER JUSTICE VERSION OR CONTRACT NUMBER FROM THIS PROMPT.

At every reuse:

1. Locate the newest completed exact Smarter Justice artifact, dedicated portal handoff, or coordination packet available in the assigned portal chat.
2. Record its exact identity and evidence classification.
3. Read its current profile schema, portal-specific profile schema, claim/create/manage link specification, security requirements, export schema, supported contract versions, compatibility policy, gates, rollout status, and owner decisions.
4. Determine the newest supported backward-compatible contract that both exact artifacts can implement safely.
5. Do not upgrade a portal contract merely because a newer schema was discussed.
6. Do not hold back a compatible portal improvement merely because another chat has not updated a summary.
7. Preserve backward compatibility or provide an explicit migration, rollout order, and rollback plan.
8. Keep live portal imports, payments, Sponsored or Featured placement, professional opportunities, reviews, appointments, confidential uploads, external AI, and deployment closed unless the exact current central and portal evidence separately supports activation and Roger approved the applicable gate.

The newest exact Smarter Justice handoff is a coordination and central-authority reference. It is not proof that the assigned portal:

- Implemented a compatible consumer
- Completed local adapter acceptance
- Completed end-to-end staging
- Imported live records
- Published live profiles
- Activated billing
- Activated Sponsored placement
- Activated opportunities
- Is deployed

Do not represent a live connection merely because the portal contains an adapter, route, fixture, sample export, schema, test, environment variable, or static status page.

The current controlling business and architecture principles remain unless Roger explicitly supersedes them:

- Basic attorney and firm profile claiming, verification, correction, and approved editing are free. Basic approved organization/service-profile claiming, authority review, correction, and removal are also free where supported.
- Full public attorney and firm profile pages live on the micro-portals. Approved organization/service-profile pages also live on the applicable micro-portal.
- Attorneys and firms centrally manage shared and approved portal-specific information through Smarter Justice. Organization/service-profile management must use the exact supported Smarter Justice contract when available; unsupported profile classes remain fail-closed rather than receiving an invented central workflow.
- Payment may support clearly labeled optional prominence and fixed-fee professional-opportunity access only after all gates are approved.
- Payment never buys identity, credentials, specialty evidence, portal eligibility, publication approval, organic rank, endorsement, favorable reviews, or guaranteed leads.
- No percentage of legal fees, settlement, recovery, refund, or outcome.
- No automatic portal-to-central write-back during the initial controlled architecture.
- Every live connection remains fail closed until exact staging and owner acceptance.

- Exact central artifact or handoff used
- Supported contract
