===============================================================================
16A. INDEPENDENT RELEASE TRUTH, NO-CIRCULAR EVIDENCE, AND SELF-REFERENCE-SAFE IDENTITY
===============================================================================

All final artifact facts must be derived independently from the frozen final
archive and its fresh extractions. Do not establish release truth by copying one
summary into another or by making tests assert values read from the same summary
under test.

INDEPENDENT FINAL EVIDENCE

After the final archive is frozen:

- compute the raw ZIP SHA-256 and exact byte size from the archive itself;
- verify CRC and safe entry structure;
- independently compute entry, file, directory, compressed-byte, and
  uncompressed-byte metrics;
- validate the self-excluding inventory from at least two independent fresh
  extractions;
- independently compute the inventory hash and canonical tree digest;
- verify staged-to-extracted equality;
- run final regression and failure tests from fresh extractions where
  practical; and
- record any environment-specific blocker without fabricating success.

Candidate evidence must remain labeled candidate evidence. A candidate build,
working tree, first extraction, earlier test run, or pre-final hash does not
become final merely because the source appears unchanged.

DETERMINISTIC AND REPRODUCIBLE BUILD CLAIMS

Use precise language:

- `DETERMINISTIC BUILD` means independent clean builds from the same declared
  source, toolchain, configuration, ordering, timestamp policy, and inputs
  produce byte-identical final archives with the same raw SHA-256 and size.
- `REPRODUCIBLE CONTENT BUILD` means independent clean builds produce the same
  canonical extracted tree, self-excluding inventory, file digests, and
  behavior, but archive bytes differ only for a disclosed and justified reason
  such as unavoidable container metadata. Do not call this byte-deterministic.
- `NOT REPRODUCED` means the comparison was not run or an authentic environment
  blocker prevented it.

When practical for a material release:

1. Create two independent candidate builds from clean staging inputs and compare
   raw ZIP bytes, entry order, timestamps, permissions, compression metadata,
   inventories, and canonical tree digests.
2. Correct unexplained nondeterminism before final acceptance.
3. After all approved content and release metadata are frozen, create two
   independent final builds and require byte identity when claiming a
   deterministic final archive.
4. If final archive bytes differ, identify the exact difference, correct it or
   classify the build honestly as reproducible-content-only or not reproduced,
   and preserve equivalent canonical-tree evidence only when it is sufficient
   for the release standard.
5. Record the exact runtime, package manager, packaging tool, locale, timezone,
   timestamp policy, environment variables, and build commands needed to repeat
   the result.

Do not use two extractions of one ZIP as proof that the ZIP build itself is
deterministic. Extraction equality proves archive consistency, not independent
build reproducibility.

EVIDENCE INVALIDATION AND REQUIRED RERUN BOUNDARY

Every test, screenshot, receipt, count, currentness review, accessibility result,
security result, integration state, build comparison, and acceptance claim must
be tied to the exact inputs it evaluated, including as applicable:

- source tree and artifact identity;
- structured and fixture data snapshot;
- content/source version and retrieval/review dates;
- schema and migration state;
- dependency and lockfile state;
- runtime, package manager, browser, packaging tool, locale, and timezone;
- configuration, environment variables, feature flags, and gate states;
- external provider/model/version and policy configuration; and
- test environment and command.

A material change to an input after evidence is produced invalidates the
affected evidence. Examples include code edits, dependency updates, data or
profile changes, legal-content or official-form updates, schema or migration
changes, route or canonical changes, CSS/layout changes affecting rendered or
accessibility acceptance, feature-flag changes, contract changes, packaging
changes, environment changes, and final-archive replacement.

Maintain a concise evidence-dependency map or equivalent traceability inside the
Release Evidence Index. After a change:

1. identify which evidence is invalidated;
2. rerun the directly affected tests and acceptance;
3. rerun connected regression where the impact can propagate;
4. use a broader regression when the impact boundary is uncertain; and
5. update the final evidence to the exact accepted artifact and environment.

Do not carry forward an earlier pass, screenshot, count, hash, browser result,
accessibility result, vulnerability result, profile-currentness review, or
integration state merely because the change appears small. Unaffected evidence
may be preserved only with an explicit reason showing that its inputs and
acceptance boundary did not change.

After the final ZIP is frozen, run all applicable final checks from fresh
extractions without modifying the archive. If any byte or accepted input changes,
discard the affected final evidence, rebuild or refreeze as needed, and repeat
the applicable acceptance and external identity process.

NO CIRCULAR RELEASE TRUTH

Do not treat any of the following as independent proof of another when they all
originate from the same unsupported value:

- release notes;
- current-truth files;
- package metadata;
- status pages;
- inventory summaries;
- tests;
- continuation prompts;
- learning packets; or
- owner-facing summaries.

At least one evidence path for each material exact fact must derive directly
from the final archive, its extracted tree, structured source data, reproduced
behavior, or an authoritative external source appropriate to the fact.

SELF-REFERENCE-SAFE FINAL ZIP IDENTITY

The raw SHA-256 and exact size of a ZIP cannot be embedded inside that same ZIP
as ordinary content and still remain the hash and size of the resulting final
archive. Therefore:

- freeze the final archive before computing its raw SHA-256 and exact size;
- do not modify, rename internally, retimestamp, recompress, or append content
  after final identity is computed;
- store the raw final ZIP identity in the owner-facing delivery record,
  external release registry, or other separately versioned record outside the
  archive;
- inside the archive, use self-excluding file inventories, inventory hashes,
  canonical tree digests, package version, and release IDs that are designed to
  be stable without pretending to be the raw ZIP hash;
- if a package includes a hash manifest that excludes itself or another defined
  record, label the exclusion precisely and do not call it the raw final ZIP
  SHA-256; and
- if any byte of the final ZIP changes, discard the prior identity and rerun the
  complete final evidence process.

BROWSER, ACCESSIBILITY, STAGING, AND ENVIRONMENT TRUTH

A unit, route, source, or static test does not prove browser acceptance. A local
browser test blocked by sandbox, policy, unavailable display, network,
credential, or infrastructure restrictions must be recorded as blocked, not
passed. Reproduce browser, accessibility, staging, backup/restore, payment,
email, storage, external-source, and deployment acceptance only in an authentic
available environment. Complete all independent safe work and provide the exact
acceptance procedure for the unavailable environment.

ADDITIONAL DURABLE, PUBLIC-EXPERIENCE, REPRODUCIBILITY, AND RESEARCH RECORDS

Every material Mode A release must also create or update, as applicable:

- `DURABLE RULES APPLIED`;
- `DURABLE RULE CHANGES SINCE SOURCE`;
- `DURABLE RULE COVERAGE AND TEST MATRIX`;
- `DURABLE RULE PROPAGATION DEBT`;
- `DURABLE RULE EXCEPTIONS AND WAIVERS`;
- `DURABLE RULE CURRENTNESS` and any `RULE DRIFT DETECTED` repair receipt;
- `CANONICAL DURABLE RULE BASELINE MANIFEST`;
- `DURABLE RULE INHERITANCE RECEIPT` and any `RULE BASELINE MISMATCH` recovery;
- `DURABLE RULE CHANGE AND BASELINE TRANSITION RECEIPT`;
- permanent rule tombstone and fork/merge reconciliation records;
- `DUAL-MASTER COMPATIBILITY RECEIPT` and any master-pair mismatch recovery;
- `MASTER-PAIR PROPAGATION TRANSACTION` where shared prompt propagation is required;
- `LAST-KNOWN-COMPATIBLE MASTER PAIR RECORD` and active-pair rollback identity;
- master-pair prepare, independent-validation, pair-validation, commit, abort,
  rollback, asymmetric-control, reciprocal-propagation, and revalidation receipts;
- `PORTAL CONTINUATION BINDING RECEIPT` and any continuation revalidation or quarantine record;
- `PORTAL REGISTRATION AND COHORT RECEIPT` and universal portal-registry state;
- `ORDINARY MATERIAL RELEASE PROFILE GROWTH RECORD` or exact narrow `PROFILE-GROWTH EXCEPTION RECEIPT`;
- `OWNER COMMERCIAL AND PUBLIC-VALUE MODEL NONREGRESSION RECEIPT`;
- `PROACTIVE CREATIVITY AND INNOVATION OPPORTUNITY RECORD`;
- `STALE PORTAL REACTIVATION AND MODERNIZATION RECORD`, or `NOT APPLICABLE WITH EVIDENCE`;
- `PUBLIC-FACING LANGUAGE AND TERMINOLOGY ACCEPTANCE`;
- `FIRST-SECONDS HOMEPAGE ACCEPTANCE`;
- `EVERY-VERSION VISUAL-SYSTEM REVIEW`;
- `GENERATED EVIDENCE REPRODUCIBILITY MANIFEST`;
- `NON-IDEMPOTENT OUTCOME REGISTER`;
- `FLAKY TEST AND VARIABLE PIPELINE QUARANTINE REGISTER`;
- `CURRENT INTERNET RESEARCH AND COMPETITIVE LEARNING REGISTER`;
- research source/currentness and lawful-use evidence;
- adopted, adapted, experimental, preserved, deferred, rejected, withdrawn, and
  not-applicable research dispositions; and
- exact continuation entries identifying unresolved rule propagation,
  generated-evidence variance, research debt, public-language defects, visual-
  system defects, tests, owner actions, and the next exact action.

Use consolidated current records where possible. Do not create duplicative
reports merely to satisfy filenames, but do not omit the required information.

17. FINAL RELEASE DELIVERABLES
===============================================================================

MODE A: the final assigned-portal ZIP is the primary code deliverable.

MODE B1: the canonical coordination record set and deduplicated owner-action
queue are the primary deliverables; do not manufacture a ZIP when no central
code changed.

MODE B2: the final Smarter Justice ZIP is the primary code deliverable.

Whenever a ZIP is delivered, the packaged artifact must be the exact artifact
tested; do not test one tree and deliver a materially different archive.

The release must also contain a concise owner-facing Release Decision Summary stating:
- What materially improved.
- Which improvements were builder-originated.
- Exact evidence completed.
- Exact capabilities still blocked, gated, simulated, or owner-dependent.
- Exact profile-growth progress and data-quality limitations.
- Exact cross-portal lessons proposed.
- Exact owner decisions still required.
- Exact rollback artifact and procedure.

The following are required evidence categories, not a mandate to create
separate files for each category. Consolidate compatible evidence into one coherent, nonduplicative set of current records, extend accurate existing records
before creating new ones, and avoid duplicative documentation.

For every category, record one disposition:

- INCLUDED;
- UPDATED EXISTING RECORD;
- NOT APPLICABLE WITH REASON;
- NO MATERIAL CHANGE WITH REASON;
- BLOCKED WITH GATE; or
- DEFERRED WITH OWNER OR EXTERNAL DEPENDENCY.

Do not create empty checklists, placeholder reports, or duplicate records merely
to increase the deliverable count. Maintain one Release Evidence Index that
maps each applicable category to the exact existing or updated file, test,
receipt, screenshot set, register, or not-applicable/blocker disposition. Each
applicable category must remain traceable and reviewable without requiring a
separate document.

    Separation Record identifying the exact portal, repository, specialty,
    profile classes, development state, launch/cohort state, deployment/live
    state, authority, and revalidation trigger.
90. Ordinary Material Release Profile Addition and Existing-Profile Enrichment
    Record identifying exact before-and-after accepted counts, new accepted
    batch, enriched existing records, final-artifact inclusion, and any narrowly
    permitted Profile-Growth Exception Receipt.
91. Owner Commercial and Public-Value Model Nonregression Receipt identifying
    the exact free-core scope, fair-use limits, high-cost-service allowance and
    cost-recovery controls, $15 monthly attorney/firm/covered-seat projections,
    free claim-control separation, one-payer/one-charge behavior, central
    authority, public copy, links, terms, tests, conflicts, and rollback.
92. Proactive Creativity and Innovation Opportunity Record identifying the
    complete opportunity scan, evidence, lawful research, candidate dispositions,
    selected connected improvements, preserved sound implementations, tests,
    rollback, and reusable cross-portal learning.
93. Stale Portal Reactivation and Modernization Record identifying every trigger,
    evidence invalidated, exact legacy base and rollback, owner reactivation when
    required, modernization baseline results, preserved capabilities, current
    risks, profile-growth application, and truthful development/launch/deployment/
    live state; use `NOT APPLICABLE WITH EVIDENCE` when no trigger exists.
94. One-Professional-Per-Canonical-Page and Entity-Uniqueness Record identifying
    every accepted individual professional class, canonical ID and URL, duplicate
    and multi-person scans, firm/practice/organization separation, relationships,
    redirects, search links, tests, conflicts, repair, and final uniqueness state.
95. Current Competitor Profile Capability-and-Quality Benchmark Record identifying
    at least three relevant official sources, access dates, observed capabilities,
    lawful-use limits, representative comparisons, matched fundamentals,
    justified superiority improvements, preserved differences, rejected copying,
    tests, and revalidation triggers.
96. Professional Profile Media Safety and Free-Control Record identifying claimant
    and entity authority, headshot/logo state, rights attestation, file validation,
    malware and decompression defenses, re-encoding, metadata removal, variants,
    accessibility, moderation, audit, cache purge, rollback, deletion, privacy,
    survivor-safety, free-control, and fallback tests.
97. Truthful Professional-Growth Entry-Point Record identifying the exact
    `Grow your law practice` or `Grow your tax practice` label, professional type,
    supporting copy, claim-versus-membership separation, centrally approved
    pricing display, no-guarantee review, mobile/accessibility acceptance,
    conversion evidence, conflicts, and rollback.
98. Network Professional Identity and Portal-Page Binding Record identifying the
    central professional ID, portal-specific canonical IDs and URLs, specialty and
    jurisdiction acceptance, field ownership, claim and seat deduplication,
    central/portal conflicts, merge/unmerge/redirect behavior, propagation, tests,
    rollback, and final identity state.
99. Publishable Profile Utility and Growth-Counting Record identifying each new
    candidate, accepted, published, enriched, suppressed, rejected, and unresolved
    record; every required utility-floor field or safe exception; rendered-page,
    search, accessibility, contact, correction/removal, provenance, currentness,
    relationship, and exact-final-artifact evidence; and the accepted count used
    for `DUR-045`.
100. Reviews, Ratings, Testimonials, Endorsements, Awards, and Featured-Placement
     Truth Record identifying every displayed or omitted trust signal, source and
     rights, calculation, moderation, material connection, incentive, sponsored or
     editorial label, dispute/correction/removal path, privacy, accessibility,
     legal/owner approval, tests, conflicts, rollback, and final state.

FINAL USER-FACING DELIVERY

For Mode A or Mode B2, when a material release is accepted, the final response
to Roger must identify the exact accepted filename, version, external raw ZIP
SHA-256, and exact byte size, then provide one direct link to that exact final
ZIP as the primary artifact. Do not provide competing candidate, draft,
intermediate, superseded, or duplicate ZIP links.

For Mode B1, provide the exact canonical coordination-record artifact or current
record location supported by the environment, plus one concise owner-action
queue and decision summary. Do not fabricate or link a code ZIP.

Keep the owner-facing response concise; complete evidence belongs in the exact
package or clearly identified coordination evidence. If the legitimate result
is no-change, do not fabricate or link a new ZIP.

Package one clean repository layout only.

Do not include:
- Secrets or credentials.
- Production data.
- Private support or identity evidence.
- node_modules.
- Caches.
- Temporary files.
- Duplicate source trees.
- Nested superseded release ZIPs.
- Unnecessary binaries.
- Misleading proof files.

Build deterministically when practical. The final packaged artifact, not merely the working folder, must pass the release checks.

18. DEFINITION OF DONE FOR THE MATERIAL NEXT VERSION — MODE A PORTAL BUILD
===============================================================================

The material next version is done only when:
- The controlling prompt input and portal-specific handoff were confirmed complete and untruncated.
- The exact starting artifact and untouched baseline are recorded.
- Every material controlling requirement has a no-loss disposition and passed final prompt regression.
- The canonical durable-rule baseline ID, ordered active IDs, semantic-capsule
  fingerprint, algorithm/canonicalization version, exact parent, inheritance
  receipt, and append-only transaction chain validate without mismatch.
- No unresolved `RULE BASELINE MISMATCH` or `RULE LINEAGE CONFLICT` supports a
  final inheritance, supersession, or canonical-successor claim.
- Superseded and withdrawn durable rules remain traceable permanent tombstones
  and no durable-rule ID has been reused.
- Every materially used Smarter Justice shared boundary has a current
  Dual-Master Compatibility Receipt with no unresolved master-pair mismatch.
- The portal-specific continuation is bound to this exact family master and
  durable baseline through a validated Portal Continuation Binding Receipt.
- The full-platform audit and maturity map are complete.
- A coherent material scope was selected from evidence.
- High-value feasible builder-originated improvements were implemented, not merely listed.
- The release was not reduced to a token revision when additional connected
  high-value work could be completed safely.
- The builder completed the full justified connected body of work and did not
  fragment it across versions merely to reduce effort.
- Every excluded connected item has a genuine blocker, dependency, safety,
  release-quality, independence, or evidence-based reason.
- Relevant accepted network practices were reviewed and adopted or adapted,
  or every material divergence was supported by a specific evidence-based
  reason.
- The builder independently discovered worthwhile opportunities beyond the
  work Roger explicitly named and implemented those supported by the selected
  release evidence; any preserved, gated, rejected, or deferred opportunity has
  a specific reason.
- All material portal facets received the release-type-appropriate examination;
  no feature, tool, workflow, or user-facing language was ignored merely
  because Roger did not mention it.
- The English experience is complete for the selected scope, alternate languages remain correctly gated, and dormant language assets are preserved.
- Public and professional experiences materially improved where evidence identified need.
- Profile handling follows the central/local authority model.
- Profile growth has a repeatable lawful source, provenance, batch, review, deduplication, currentness, and correction method.
- Canonical attorney and firm profile behavior—and applicable organization/service-profile behavior—is verified to the highest environment genuinely available.
- Backend, security, privacy, accessibility, reliability, owner operations, and release quality were addressed rather than deferred wholesale.
- Tests were preserved and strengthened.
- Every claim of readiness has matching evidence.
- Closed gates remain closed unless separately accepted and approved.
- The existing Cross-Portal Learning Packet is updated with any material lesson
  or an honest no-new-lesson entry, and Candidate Shared Standards exist only
  where a reusable proposal is actually supported.
- The Next Version Improvement List preserves all unresolved truth.
- The final deterministic or reproducibly built ZIP passes fresh-extraction validation and the recorded test suite.
- The launch-tier/capability matrix, environment, path-scoped D0-D5 state matrix, and every active or closed
  live-service gate are exact.
- Every launch-critical real-world dependency has either a verified Owner
  Assistance Request outcome or an explicit owner-deferred fallback tier.
- No secret entered chat, source, evidence, logs, screenshots, tests, or the ZIP.
- The product row and deduplicated owner-action queue in the five-platform
  launch register are current, or an exact fail-closed `PENDING CANONICAL
  RECEIPT` proposal is preserved when the coordinator is temporarily unavailable.
- The owner receives one exact artifact through one direct final ZIP link and a truthful decision summary.

MODE B2 SMARTER JUSTICE CENTRAL-BUILD DEFINITION OF DONE

A Mode B2 material next version is complete only when:

- the exact Smarter Justice starting artifact and untouched baseline are
  recorded;
- the current central continuation or build mission is complete and untruncated;
- all central requirements have no-loss dispositions and prompt regression;
- central identity, authentication, claimant verification, firm/office/seat
  authority, membership, billing, entitlements, support, complaints, contracts,
  monitoring, incidents, backup/restore, and rollback were examined at the
  required depth;
- the strongest justified connected central scope was implemented and exact-
  tested without portal repository changes;
- every affected portal contract, path-scoped D0-D5 state matrix, suppression path, failure state,
  and rollback boundary is truthful and product-specific;
- the exact Smarter Justice ZIP is deterministic or honestly reproducible,
  independently fresh-extracted, and tied to the release evidence;
- D4, D5, vendor, and live-gate acceptance is bound to the exact candidate,
  environment, migration, provider, feature-flag, and nonsecret configuration
  state;
- the canonical five-platform register and owner-action queue are current and
  receipted;
- no portal acceptance was copied or inferred from central implementation;
- all unproven central and portal live gates remain closed; and
- Roger receives one direct link to the exact Smarter Justice ZIP plus one
  concise coordination and owner-action summary.

MODE B1 COORDINATION-ONLY DEFINITION OF DONE

A Mode B1 cycle is complete only when:

- the canonical register and owner-action queue have one identified single
  writer and current receipted version;
- every affected product row identifies exact artifact evidence, the launch-
  tier/capability matrix, path-scoped D0-D5 state matrix, environment, active/
  closed gates, rollback, and evidence date;
- duplicate requests are merged by stable deduplication key without losing
  product-specific acceptance;
- every actionable owner or external dependency has a complete secure request,
  dependency sequence, fallback, and revalidation date;
- conflicting local and canonical states are reconciled or fail closed;
- shared infrastructure actions link to separate product acceptance receipts;
- no portal repository was modified;
- no unsupported global state was inferred from one product; and
- Roger receives one concise current action queue and decision summary.

A release may still be materially complete while external production, legal, payment, SMTP, database, staging, or live-integration acceptance remains blocked, provided the builder completed all safe work available, kept gates closed, and recorded the blockers precisely.

The release is also not done until:

- every applicable active durable rule has an exact coverage disposition and
  semantic no-loss review;
- no unresolved `RULE DRIFT DETECTED` affects the candidate;
- public-facing terminology, emails, notifications, exports, metadata, and
  user-visible status/error text pass plain-language review without internal or
  developer leakage;
- the first-seconds homepage and the complete visual system receive the required
  every-version review and human acceptance appropriate to the release;
- generated evidence is honestly classified, repeated at the required risk
  level, and free of unresolved material non-idempotent outcomes used for
  acceptance;
- flaky or variable tests and pipelines are quarantined rather than converted
  into evidence through reruns;
- current internet and lawful competitive research is complete at the release-
  appropriate depth, with source/currentness evidence and target-specific
  dispositions; and
- the continuation records durable-rule currentness, propagation debt,
  reproducibility state, research state, public-language/design state, and the
  exact next action.

===============================================================================
19. STOP CONDITIONS
===============================================================================

Stop and reconcile before continuing only when:
- The active mode or repository authority is ambiguous for the affected action.
- Canonical and product-local launch or owner-action records conflict for an
  action that would open a gate.
- A D4, D5, or live-gate acceptance cannot be bound to the exact candidate,
  environment, migration state, and nonsecret configuration manifest.
- The candidate ZIP does not match the expected authoritative artifact and no newer exact artifact was deliberately supplied.
- The repository root or inventory is materially inconsistent.
- A secret, credential, unsafe path, unapproved symlink, malicious file, production dump, or unexplained binary is found.
- A migration risks irreversible loss without rollback.
- The portal architecture would bypass Smarter Justice central identity, claim, credential, membership, billing, or entitlement authority.
- A feature would bypass verification, authorization, privacy, suppression, or publication controls.
- The handoff contract is materially incompatible.
- A legal or public claim would be misleading.
- The exact rollback is unknown for a deployment-affecting change.

Do not stop because:
- The platform has many improvement opportunities.
- Some external credentials are unavailable.
- Some production acceptance remains blocked.
- The build is complex.
- Roger did not specify every design or engineering detail.

In those situations, build and verify everything that can be completed safely, preserve closed gates, and record remaining blockers honestly.

===============================================================================
20. FINAL MODE-SPECIFIC MANDATE
===============================================================================

BEGIN NOW.

Treat this prompt as the explicit command to perform the work authorized by the
active mode in the current working session.

MODE A or MODE B2 required default path is:

1. verify the exact authoritative artifact;
2. perform the required complete-platform scan and deep audit;
3. select the complete justified connected release scope;
4. implement the work;
5. regression-test and exact-test the candidate;
6. package and independently fresh-extract it;
7. verify deterministic or reproducible artifact truth;
8. select and prepare the strongest safe launch tier;
9. issue and verify every actionable Owner Assistance Request without exposing secrets;
10. complete accepted environment-specific and post-deployment checks when deployment is approved; and
11. deliver the active-mode product ZIP and exact launch/gate truth.

MODE B1 required path is:

1. verify the canonical coordination inputs;
2. reconcile the five product rows and dependencies;
3. deduplicate and sequence owner actions;
4. prepare every actionable secure request and fallback;
5. receipt verified state transitions;
6. keep unsupported gates closed; and
7. deliver the current coordination records and concise owner queue without an
   artificial source-code release.

Do not ask whether to begin. Do not end after the audit, opportunity register,
scope record, or plan. Do not wait for another owner message before
implementation.

Only when the exceptional no-change evidence burden is completely satisfied
may the builder preserve the authoritative artifact and deliver a no-change
audit instead of a new ZIP.

Do not merely restate this prompt.
Do not produce only a plan, audit, scope proposal, recommendation list, or
status explanation when the exact artifact is available and portal-side
implementation can proceed.
Do not make cosmetic changes for the sake of change.
Do not wait for Roger to identify every opportunity, defect, best practice, or next improvement.
Do not remove working functionality without a demonstrated benefit.
Do not weaken truth, safety, privacy, security, accessibility, or rollback controls.
In Mode A, do not modify Smarter Justice or another portal repository. In Mode B, do not modify a portal repository; modify Smarter Justice only in B2 when the current central mission authorizes it.
Do not deploy without explicit approval.

Use professional judgment, creativity, evidence, and disciplined engineering
to make the assigned portal materially better through the complete justified
body of connected work that can be responsibly finished and exact-tested,
while preserving complete-platform awareness and every applicable standing
safeguard.

Do not deliver a tiny revision merely because it is easier to finish. Complete
the work correctly and comprehensively within the genuine safety, dependency,
repository, owner-approval, and exact-release boundaries.

Build a stronger public legal starting point.
Build exceptional attorney and firm profiles and, where applicable, exceptional public-safe organization/service-provider profiles.
Build a fast, lawful, source-tracked profile-growth operation.
Build better search and profile relationships.
Build safer and clearer professional acquisition.
Build a reliable read-only Smarter Justice integration.
Build owner and staff operations that reduce developer dependence.
Build mobile, accessibility, security, privacy, reliability, and release quality into the product.
Review and apply relevant accepted lessons from Smarter Justice and the other portals, and share every new reusable lesson so the full network learns and improves together. Diverge only for a specific recorded reason.
Build the complete English experience first and keep future languages safely gated until their separate acceptance phase.

Package, fresh-extract, exact-test, and deliver one honest next-version ZIP
unless the exceptional no-change burden is fully proven. Do not fabricate a
release artifact, but do not use that prohibition as permission to avoid a
justified build.

Do not end the build with only recommendations when safe material improvements can be implemented in the current work. Do not ask Roger to choose among ordinary product, engineering, design, data, or testing decisions that the builder can resolve responsibly from evidence. Escalate only true owner decisions, legal approvals, irreversible risk, missing authoritative artifacts, production credentials, or stop-condition conflicts.

21. REPEATED BUILD-CYCLE CONTINUITY AND SELF-UPDATING HANDOFF
===============================================================================

Because this prompt may be reused many times, every release must leave the next builder with current truth.

Every final package must update:

- Current authoritative version
- Exact artifact identity
- Repository root
- Runtime and dependencies
- Current deployment truth
- Current production truth
- Current profile and firm counts
- Current geographic and specialty coverage
- Current Smarter Justice coordination artifact
- Current supported handoff contract
- Current D0–D5 integration maturity
- Current launch-tier/capability matrix and exact accepted scope
- Current Owner Assistance Requests, dependencies, safe confirmations, and verification states
- Current hosting, domain, DNS/TLS, registry, database, email, payment, monitoring, storage, AI/OCR, accessibility-review, staging, and production connection state without secret values
- Current feature gates
- Current rollback
- Current blockers
- Current owner decisions
- Current continuation prompt
- Current complete Next Version Improvement List
- Current Cross-Portal Learning Packet
- Current profile-source and source-yield records
- Current profile candidate backlog
- Current legal-content freshness record
- Current English-first language state, dormant assets, and future activation gates
- Current Builder Opportunity and Best-Practice Decision Register
- Current accessibility and device record
- Current security and privacy record
- Current monitoring, support, backup, restore, and incident record
- Current-use truth-record inventory and Current Truth Reconciliation Report
- Current exact rollback target, compatibility, known limitations, and tested procedure
- Current learning lineage, source-version revalidation, target acceptance, and propagation state
- Current self-reference-safe external final ZIP identity record
- Current atomic recoverable checkpoint identity, workspace state, and recovery command
- Current tamper-evident evidence manifest and decision/receipt chain
- Current acceptance-freeze state, approved change records, and reacceptance requirements

The portal's continuation prompt should not hard-code the next version as an eternal base. It must say:

- Use the newest exact completed artifact uploaded in the dedicated chat.
- Reverify it in the current builder chat at the depth required by the next task.
- Distinguish prior-chat recorded evidence from current-chat exact inspection and reproduction.
- Continue its artifact and learning lineage.
- Revalidate older learning against the newest source version before propagation.
- Do not skip intervening releases.
- Do not rely on stale version facts from a reusable instruction.
- Include `NEWEST ARTIFACT LEARNING APPLIED` and the current propagation state.
- Preserve the current-use truth inventory and exact accepted rollback target.

When a release supersedes a prior release:

- Preserve the prior artifact identity.
- Preserve its tests and limitations.
- Mark it superseded.
- Do not rewrite it as if it had the new source.
- Identify the exact rollback relationship.
- Carry unresolved items forward.

A new chat must be able to determine the current base without relying on Roger's memory.

===============================================================================
21A. COMPLETE CURRENT-TRUTH, CURRENT-USE RECORD, AND ROLLBACK RECONCILIATION
===============================================================================

Every material release must reconcile current truth across the complete set of
records and surfaces that a builder, owner, staff member, public user,
professional, test, status endpoint, or future continuation chat may reasonably
treat as current.

CURRENT-USE VERSUS HISTORICAL CLASSIFICATION

Classify each relevant record as:

- CURRENT AUTHORITATIVE;
- CURRENT DERIVED;
- CURRENT OPERATIONAL;
- HISTORICAL;
- SUPERSEDED;
- FIXTURE OR SAMPLE;
- GENERATED AND REPRODUCIBLE;
- STALE;
- UNKNOWN; or
- NOT APPLICABLE.

Do not rewrite historical release evidence to make it appear current. Do not
allow a historical version, count, portal set, rollback target, profile total,
relationship total, route total, test total, readiness state, or integration
state to remain in an unversioned or current-use record without an explicit
historical label.

MANDATORY CURRENT-TRUTH SCAN

Search and reconcile, as applicable:

- package metadata and runtime requirements;
- current artifact and release registries;
- control-center, dashboard, status, readiness, launch, and deployment records;
- data inventories and current profile, firm, office, organization,
  relationship, search, claim, publication, language, and integration counts;
- current profile-governance, relationship-governance, source-currentness,
  support, incident, backup, restore, and rollback documents;
- current UI copy, public status pages, owner/staff views, APIs, generated
  reports, exports, and machine-readable status files;
- current continuation prompts, handoffs, Master Rules Pack references,
  Cross-Portal Learning Packets, and Next Version Improvement Lists;
- current tests and fixtures that assert present truth rather than historical
  compatibility; and
- stale three-portal or other superseded portfolio language when the current
  coordinated four-portal/five-platform truth applies.

A primary release summary, one passing test, or one registry file does not prove
that all current-use records agree. Tests must validate every material
current-use source of truth or a canonical generated source from which those
records are deterministically derived.

TEMPORAL TRUTH AND CURRENTNESS SEMANTICS

Do not use one ambiguous `date`, `updated`, `current`, or file-modification
timestamp for different meanings. Distinguish, where applicable:

- `source_published_at` or the official source's effective date;
- `source_retrieved_at`;
- `source_reviewed_at`;
- `effective_as_of`;
- `expires_at` or `reverify_by`;
- `record_created_at` and `record_updated_at`;
- `release_built_at`;
- `release_accepted_at`;
- `deployed_at`; and
- `last_observed_at` for operational evidence.

Use ISO 8601 timestamps with an explicit timezone or UTC offset for
machine-readable records. Use America/New_York for owner-facing build and
release dates unless another jurisdiction or system requires a different
explicit timezone.

A filesystem modification time, ZIP entry timestamp, upload time, generated
report time, or package-version date is not proof that the underlying legal,
professional, agency, form, hotline, pricing, profile, or operational fact was
reviewed or remains current. A retrieval date is not automatically the source's
effective date. A future planned date is not an achieved state.

Resolve relative language such as `today`, `current`, `latest`, `recent`, or
`this week` into an exact date or evidence window when it affects a material
claim, gate, source-currentness decision, deadline, or release record. Apply
source-specific revalidation intervals and fail closed when required current
truth cannot be established.

CURRENT-TRUTH REGRESSION CONTROL

Add or strengthen regression tests so a future release fails when material
current-use records disagree about:

- current version and exact artifact lineage;
- portal set or portfolio coordination scope;
- counts and relationships;
- routes, tools, forms, profiles, and language state;
- test totals and release evidence;
- Smarter Justice contract and integration maturity;
- deployment, staging, launch, outreach, payment, profile-publication, and
  opportunity gates;
- currentness and source dates; or
- rollback target and procedure.

Do not validate one current summary merely by comparing it with another summary
that copied the same unsupported value. Recompute from the exact source,
structured data, final archive, or independently reproduced behavior.

ROLLBACK TARGET AND PROCEDURE

The current rollback target must have:

- exact filename, version, SHA-256, size, repository root, and inventory;
- accepted release state and known limitations;
- compatibility with current data, schemas, contracts, routes, and operations;
- a documented reason when it is not the immediate prior accepted release;
- a tested extraction and rollback procedure at the highest environment
  honestly available;
- data and migration reversal or forward-recovery instructions;
- last-known-good and suppression behavior where relevant; and
- owner/staff instructions that do not point to an obsolete, unsafe, missing,
  or unrelated artifact.

An older artifact may be the accepted rollback only when evidence shows it is a
safer compatible fallback than the immediate predecessor. If no valid rollback
exists, keep deployment or irreversible migration gates closed and record the
exact remediation.

CURRENT TRUTH RECONCILIATION REPORT

Every material release must include or update one concise, nonduplicative
Current Truth Reconciliation Report that records:

- the current-use record inventory;
- stale or conflicting facts found;
- corrections made;
- historical records intentionally preserved;
- independent derivation method for material metrics;
- regression coverage added;
- exact rollback target and acceptance result; and
- unresolved truth or rollback blockers.

===============================================================================
22. PROFILE EXPANSION RELEASE GATE
===============================================================================

Add a dedicated PROFILE EXPANSION release gate.

The gate may be PASS only when:

- Current before counts are exact.
- Lawful sources were reviewed.
- Source admissibility is recorded.
- Profile-growth work was performed.
- All accepted records have stable IDs.
- Duplicate and ambiguity checks ran.
- Specialty evidence was evaluated.
- Firm and office relationships were evaluated.
- Field-level provenance exists.
- Last-retrieved and last-reviewed dates exist.
- Every public accepted record has a complete canonical page or a documented gated private state.
- Every public accepted record is searchable.
- Every public accepted record has claim, correction, and removal paths.
- Every accepted record is included in the final exact ZIP.
- Search and page performance were tested with the new volume.
- Exact before-and-after metrics are recorded.
- Rejected and held records are recorded honestly.
- A future refresh schedule exists.

The gate may be PASS WITH DOCUMENTED PROFILE-GROWTH EXCEPTION only when:

- the outcome is a narrowly classified non-ordinary emergency, rollback,
  recovery, artifact-integrity correction, or genuine no-change audit;
- the release contains no broader ordinary material product work;
- exact evidence proves why profile addition would conflict with the narrow
  purpose or safety boundary;
- existing profile systems received the applicable regression and currentness
  review, and existing profiles were refreshed or enriched when safely possible;
- the next lawful profile-growth action and revalidation trigger are recorded;
  and
- Roger is not misled into thinking profile growth occurred.

`No safe qualified additions were found` is not by itself an exception for an
ordinary material release. Lack of time, competing work, difficulty, release
size, or failure to search must fail this gate.

The gate must FAIL when:

- Profiles were merely discussed.
- Candidate files were created but not integrated.
- Unsupported records were added.
- Duplicates were knowingly created.
- Specialty was inferred only from licensure.
- Source use is unlawful or unclear.
- Public pages are incomplete.
- Search excludes the new records.
- Counts are inflated.
- Fixtures are counted as real data.
- The final ZIP differs from the tested profile data.

===============================================================================
23. PORTAL-SPECIFIC PROFILE-ACQUISITION STRATEGIES
===============================================================================

Apply the assigned portal's strategy.

DIVORCE LAW AID

Prioritize attorneys and firms with explicit evidence for:

- Divorce
- Matrimonial law
- Separation
- Child custody
- Parenting and visitation
- Child support
- Spousal support
- Property division
- Prenuptial and postnuptial agreements where supported
- Family-law mediation or collaborative practice where supported
- Related family-law litigation or appeals where supported

Do not infer Divorce Law Aid eligibility merely from a general family-law label when the portal requires a narrower matter type.

High-yield clusters may include:

- Matrimonial and family-law firms
- Courthouse-area firms
- Bar matrimonial and family-law sections
- Multi-attorney firms with individual biographies
- Owner-approved attorney outreach cohorts
- Downtown Brooklyn, broader Brooklyn, Manhattan, Queens, Bronx, Staten Island, Long Island, Westchester, and approved nearby markets

ESTATE LAW AID

Prioritize attorneys and firms with explicit evidence for:

- Estate planning
- Wills
- Trusts
- Probate
- Estate administration
- Trust administration
- Guardianship
- Elder-law-related estate planning where supported
- Special-needs planning
- Estate and trust litigation where supported
- Estate and gift tax where supported
- Business succession where supported

High-yield clusters may include:

- Estate planning and elder-law firms
- Probate and Surrogate's Court practice firms
- Bar trusts and estates sections
- Multi-attorney firms with practice rosters
- Courthouse and professional-building clusters
- Owner-approved outreach cohorts
- New York, New Jersey, Connecticut, and other approved markets

PERSONAL INJURY LAW AID

Prioritize attorneys and firms with explicit evidence for:

- Vehicle accidents
- Truck accidents
- Motorcycle accidents
- Bicycle and pedestrian injuries
- Rideshare and delivery-vehicle accidents
- Premises liability
- Slip-and-fall and trip-and-fall matters
- Negligent security
- Defective products
- Dog bites where supported
- Construction or worksite third-party injury matters
- Wrongful death
- Other general negligence injuries

Do not absorb:

- Workers' Compensation
- Medical Malpractice

Use transparent cross-portal handoffs when evidence indicates a separate legal path.

High-yield clusters may include:

- Personal-injury firms
- Trial and plaintiff-side firms
- Multi-office firms
- Courthouse-area firms
- Professional-building clusters
- Bar trial-lawyer or tort sections
- Owner-approved outreach cohorts
- New York City and other approved markets

DOMESTIC VIOLENCE AID

Prioritize attorneys and firms with explicit evidence for:

- Orders of protection and family-offense matters.
- Domestic-violence-related family law.
- Divorce, custody, support, and related safety-sensitive proceedings.
- Crime-victim representation or navigation where supported.
- Housing, employment, immigration, disability, civil-rights, and benefits intersections where supported.
- Trauma-informed or survivor-centered services only when the source explicitly supports that description.

Also prioritize source-supported profiles for:

- Legal-aid organizations.
- Domestic-violence advocates and victim-service organizations.
- Nonprofits and community organizations.
- Public agencies.
- Shelters and service providers using public-safe contact information only.
- Prevention and education organizations.
- Stop Sign Project participants or partners only when the relationship is supported and approved.

High-yield sources and clusters may include:

- Official government victim-service and legal-aid directories.
- Organization-controlled websites.
- Public court and agency directories.
- Bar family-law, matrimonial, criminal, housing, immigration, employment, and public-interest sections where relevant.
- Multi-attorney firms with explicit domestic-violence-related practice evidence.
- Reviewed nonprofit and service-provider directories with lawful reuse.
- Owner-approved outreach cohorts.
- New York City and other approved markets.

Never infer:

- Confidential shelter location.
- Current bed or service availability.
- Emergency response.
- Eligibility.
- Quality.
- Endorsement.
- Government affiliation.
- Trauma-informed status.
- Language.
- Safe contact method.
- Acceptance of new clients.

OTHER REGISTERED SMARTER JUSTICE LEGAL MICRO-PORTAL

For any assigned portal not named in the four legacy strategy subsections above,
create a `PORTAL-SPECIFIC PROFILE ACQUISITION STRATEGY` before accepting a batch.
It must identify:

- exact specialty and matter taxonomy;
- explicit adjacent specialties that belong in other portals;
- attorney, firm, organization, service-provider, agency, expert, or other
  profile classes lawfully supported by the portal;
- jurisdiction and geography;
- specialty evidence required at firm and individual level;
- lawful high-yield official, organization-controlled, bar, court, agency, and
  other source classes;
- source-use rights, prohibited sources, and manual-review requirements;
- privacy, safety, confidential-location, eligibility, availability, and
  endorsement boundaries;
- entity-resolution, office, firm-roster, cross-portal, correction/removal, and
  currentness rules;
- canonical page, search, claim, publication, and testing requirements; and
- exact batch goal, acceptance metrics, rejection reasons, and refresh schedule.

Do not infer specialty merely from general licensure, broad marketing language,
firm association, geography, or a neighboring practice area. Do not copy the
initial four portals' taxonomy into another specialty. Use transparent cross-
portal routing when the evidence belongs elsewhere.

For all portals:

- A firm-wide practice page may support firm eligibility.
- Individual attorney eligibility should use individual evidence where reasonably available.
- Do not assign every attorney in a firm to every firm practice automatically.
- Preserve a review state when the firm-level evidence is strong but individual evidence is incomplete.
- Add both attorneys and firms when the sources support both.
- For Domestic Violence Aid, add approved organizations/services only when profile class, source authority, public-safe contact, confidential-location review, correction/removal rights, and publication state are complete.
- Do not add only a firm shell when its public attorney roster can be lawfully and accurately resolved during the same build.
- Do not add only attorneys without resolving the firm relationship when reliable firm evidence is available.

24. EVERY ACCEPTED PROFILE MUST BECOME A COMPLETE PRODUCT SURFACE
===============================================================================

A new profile is not complete merely because a JSON record exists.

For every accepted public attorney profile:

- Stable portal attorney ID
- Stable central professional ID when known
- One canonical public HTTPS URL
- Complete approved public fields
- Portal-specific practice evidence
- Firm and office relationship
- Firm-page link where applicable
- Claim state
- Verification state
- Source provenance
- Last retrieved
- Last reviewed
- Currentness state
- Claim link
- Correction link
- Removal or suppression path
- Manage link to Smarter Justice after claim
- Search-index entry
- Sitemap and canonical handling where appropriate
- Structured data only when accurate
- Mobile, tablet, desktop, keyboard, zoom, and screen-reader preparation
- Useful missing-field behavior
- No unsupported claims
- No duplicate canonical page

For every accepted public firm profile:

- Stable portal firm ID
- Stable central firm ID when known
- One canonical public HTTPS URL
- Firm identity and public business information
- Offices
- Supported portal-specific practice areas
- Languages where supported
- Verified attorney roster
- Links to canonical attorney pages
- Attorney relationship currentness
- Claim and authority state
- Source provenance
- Last retrieved
- Last reviewed
- Correction
- Removal or suppression
- Search-index entry
- Mobile and accessibility acceptance
- Sponsored label only when separately active and approved


For every accepted public Domestic Violence Aid organization/service-provider profile:

- Stable portal organization/service ID.
- Stable central organization ID when the exact supported central contract provides one.
- Exact profile class.
- One canonical public HTTPS URL.
- Approved name and legal/status description only when supported.
- Public-safe website, phone, email, and service area.
- Public location only when safe, authorized, and sourced.
- Confidential/nonpublic location suppression.
- Supported services, populations served, languages, and accessibility.
- Source provenance.
- Last retrieved and last reviewed.
- Claimed/unclaimed and authority-review state.
- Correction, removal, urgent safety-suppression, and restoration.
- Search-index entry when public.
- Stop Sign Project relationship only when supported.
- Mobile and accessibility acceptance.
- No unsupported availability, eligibility, capacity, emergency response, endorsement, quality, government status, or outcome claim.

A profile may remain private or review-pending when evidence is insufficient for public display. Do not force public publication to increase counts.

Every accepted profile must be tested through:

- Data validation
- Public rendering
- Search
- Canonical URL
- Claim or create path
- Correction
- Suppression
- Relationship links
- Export/import behavior where applicable
- Last-known-good
- Rollback

===============================================================================
26. ENTITY RESOLUTION, FIRM ROSTERS, LIFECYCLE, AND CURRENTNESS
===============================================================================

Handle:

- Same-name attorneys
- Name changes
- Middle-name and suffix variation
- Registration-number variation
- Multiple offices
- Multiple firms
- Former firm relationships
- Firm aliases
- Firm mergers
- Firm closures
- Office moves
- Broken websites
- Suspended or inactive registrations
- Deceased professionals
- Duplicate seeded profiles
- Duplicate central IDs
- Portal-local versus central identity conflicts
- Conflicting specialty evidence
- Stale bios
- Retired attorneys
- Government or judicial role changes where relevant

Never silently merge ambiguous records.

Every merge and split must record:

- Source entities
- Destination entities
- Reason
- Evidence
- Operator
- Date
- Public URL handling
- Redirect handling
- Central ID impact
- Rollback

CURRENTNESS

Each profile and source should have:

- Last retrieved
- Last source check
- Last staff review
- Last professional confirmation
- Last central update
- Last portal import
- Currentness state
- Next review date
- Broken-source state
- Credential recheck state
- Firm relationship recheck state
- Public contact recheck state

Every build must refresh a reasonable number of existing records as well as add new profiles.

Prioritize refresh for:

- High-traffic pages
- Oldest records
- Broken sources
- Unclaimed profiles
- Profiles with disputed or weak firm relationships
- Suspended or inactive status
- Recently claimed profiles
- Paid-growth candidates
- Outreach targets
- Profiles affected by source changes

===============================================================================
27. SEARCH, SEO, DIRECTORY QUALITY, AND PROFILE-GROWTH SCALE
===============================================================================

Profile growth is useful only when public users can find and understand the profiles.

Search should support, where evidence and portal scope justify:

- Attorney
- Firm
- Name
- City
- County
- State
- ZIP or approximate radius
- Office
- Portal-specific practice area
- Matter type
- Language
- Firm roster
- Claimed state when useful
- Verified state when accurately defined

Do not make “verified” a ranking shortcut unless the meaning is transparent and legally appropriate.

Organic search ordering must be neutral and documented.

Possible neutral factors may include:

- Exact query match
- Specialty relevance
- Geography
- Complete supported public data
- Currentness
- Stable deterministic tie-breaker

Payment, personal preference, unsupported quality judgment, or hidden sponsorship must not alter organic order.

Sponsored placement:

- Remains separate
- Requires current central entitlement
- Requires legal and owner gate
- Must be clearly labeled
- Must not remove useful organic results
- Must not claim superiority

Every build with profile growth must test:

- Search performance with the new data volume
- Pagination or show-more
- Filter preservation
- Canonical links
- Firm-to-attorney navigation
- Attorney-to-firm navigation
- Zero results
- Stale and suppressed exclusion
- Duplicate prevention
- Mobile
- Keyboard
- Zoom
- Screen-reader preparation
- SEO canonical and sitemap behavior
- Structured data validity
- No thin profile pages
- No duplicate or orphan pages

Do not publish mass-generated geography or profile pages that lack useful supported content.

===============================================================================
28. CLAIM, CENTRAL EDITING, PAID-GROWTH, AND PROFESSIONAL CONVERSION
===============================================================================

The public portal is the discovery and full profile surface.

Smarter Justice is the central professional control surface.

Existing seeded profile journey:

1. Public user or attorney opens the canonical portal profile.
2. Attorney selects “Claim this profile.”
3. Portal creates minimum safe signed or tamper-resistant context.
4. Attorney reaches the newest current Smarter Justice claim flow.
5. Account, email, identity, authority, and claim review occur centrally.
6. Approved control is granted centrally.
7. Basic approved editing is free.
8. Protected and specialty changes follow review.
9. Approved portal projection returns through the current read-only contract.
10. The portal updates the same canonical page.
11. “Manage profile” returns centrally.

No-seed journey:

1. Attorney selects “Create a professional profile.”
2. Portal sends portal context and create intent.
3. Central private draft is created.
4. Identity, credentials, specialty, firm, and portal participation are reviewed separately.
5. Public projection occurs only after approval.

Conversion requirements:

- Explain free basic profile control clearly.
- Do not imply payment is required to correct the record.
- Do not imply payment creates verification.
- Do not imply payment guarantees publication.
- Explain optional paid products only when the current gate permits.
- Use QR-friendly mobile forms.
- Use browser autofill appropriately.
- Avoid unnecessary fields.
- Preserve progress safely.
- Provide support and recovery.
- Show clear status.
- Avoid dark patterns.
- Do not pressure professionals with fake deadlines.

Paid products remain central and may include:

- Clearly labeled Sponsored or Featured prominence
- Enhanced visibility separate from organic results
- Fixed-fee opportunity access
- Firm and seat products
- Approved analytics

The portal must rely on current server-authoritative entitlement state, not browser state.

===============================================================================
29. SOURCE RIGHTS, LEGAL COMPLIANCE, PROFESSIONAL RESPONSIBILITY, AND PUBLIC TRUTH
===============================================================================

Every profile-growth source must be reviewed for:

- Public availability
- Terms
- License
- API conditions
- Robots policy
- Rate limits
- Attribution
- Data reuse
- Retention
- Correction
- Removal
- Security
- Privacy
- Jurisdiction
- Freshness
- Permitted automation

Do not copy:

- Competitor profile text without permission
- Competitor ratings
- Competitor reviews
- Proprietary badges
- Proprietary rankings
- Copyrighted biographies beyond permitted factual use
- Photos or logos without rights
- Private or access-controlled data

Attorney advertising and directory compliance must consider:

- Truthful identity
- Supported specialty
- No misleading superiority
- No implied endorsement
- No fake membership
- No fake availability
- No guaranteed outcome
- Clear Sponsored labels
- No disguised ads
- Required state disclaimers
- Record retention where applicable
- Correction and removal
- Qualified legal review before live monetization

Professional opportunities must consider:

- User consent
- Minimum necessary information
- Conflicts
- Attorney choice
- No automatic relationship
- Fixed-fee platform or access structure
- No percentage of legal fees or outcomes
- No nonlawyer fee sharing
- No guaranteed response
- Support
- Audit
- Jurisdiction-specific legal review

The portal must never state that it is:

- A law firm
- An attorney
- A court
- A government agency
- A bar association
- A licensing authority
- A guaranteed referral service
- A guaranteed lead service

30. OPERATIONS, SUPPORT, COMMUNICATIONS, FRAUD, AND BUSINESS CONTINUITY
===============================================================================

Each portal must be operable after profile counts grow.

OWNER AND STAFF QUEUES

- Source review
- Batch review
- Duplicate review
- Ambiguous identity
- Specialty evidence
- Firm relationship
- Broken source
- Stale profile
- Claim conflict
- Correction
- Suppression
- Removal
- Restoration
- Import rejection
- Import conflict
- Sponsored display
- Opportunity
- Support
- Security incident

SUPPORT

Define routing for:

- Profile accuracy
- Claim
- Firm authority
- Attorney affiliation
- Search
- Public legal tool
- Accessibility
- Privacy
- Security
- Central account
- Billing
- Sponsored placement
- Opportunity
- Portal import
- Suppression
- Removal

COMMUNICATIONS

Transactional communications may include:

- Claim handoff
- Claim received
- More information required
- Claim approved or rejected
- Profile update status
- Firm relationship dispute
- Correction status
- Suppression status
- Portal publication status
- Import failure support

Central account, security, billing, and entitlement email should remain with Smarter Justice unless the exact architecture says otherwise.

Never email or log:

- Passwords
- MFA secrets
- Private evidence
- Full payment data
- Confidential legal narratives
- Uploaded legal documents
- Unnecessary personal data

FRAUD AND ABUSE

Protect against:

- False claims
- Attorney impersonation
- Firm impersonation
- Spam profile creation
- Source tampering
- Duplicate profile attacks
- Scraping abuse
- Search denial of service
- Signed-state replay
- Import replay
- Stale overwrite
- Suppression bypass
- Unauthorized owner actions
- Paid-label removal
- Opportunity scraping or resale

BUSINESS CONTINUITY

When Smarter Justice is unavailable:

- Public portal content may remain available.
- Existing public profiles may remain according to last-known-good policy.
- Claim and manage actions show truthful unavailability.
- Do not create local credentials.
- Do not fabricate completion.
- Preserve support routes.

When the portal is unavailable:

- Central account and editing remain available.
- Publication is marked pending.
- No immediate update is promised.
- Staff sees the outage.
- Safe retry and reconciliation remain possible.

===============================================================================
31. PRECISE STATUS AND TRUTH CLASSIFICATION
===============================================================================

Every release must distinguish:

COMPLETED AND EXACT VERIFIED

- Exists in final packaged artifact
- Appropriate tests passed
- Exact evidence recorded

IMPLEMENTED BUT NEEDS REVIEW OR CONFIGURATION

- Source exists
- Full acceptance is not complete

LOCALLY VERIFIED

- Tested in the portal's local environment

BROWSER VERIFIED

- Tested in real browser conditions with recorded evidence

SMARTER JUSTICE INTEGRATION MATURITY — COMPLETE PATH-SCOPED D0-D5 LADDER

Use this ladder only for a specifically identified portal-to-Smarter-Justice
integration path or contract boundary. It does not replace the separate feature,
publication, payment, outreach, accessibility, browser, legal-review,
deployment, or language-state gates.

Before assigning D0-D5, record an `INTEGRATION_BOUNDARY_ID` identifying the
product, producer, consumer, contract/version, direction, capability/path, and
environment. A product may have different states for claim handoff, profile
projection, suppression, membership, billing, support, or another path.

Do not report the product-wide state as the maximum accepted path. Use the full
path matrix. When a one-line summary is unavoidable, report the lowest current
state among the launch-required paths and name any higher accepted paths
separately. D4 or D5 for one boundary does not prove D4 or D5 for another.

D0 NOT CURRENTLY VERIFIED

- No current exact producer/consumer contract and compatibility result has been
  established for the assigned portal build.
- Existing code, fixtures, routes, or historical records may exist, but no
  present integration-maturity claim is permitted.

D1 CONTRACT IDENTIFIED AND COMPATIBILITY ASSESSED

- Exact portal and Smarter Justice artifacts or controlling handoffs are
  identified.
- Contract version, schema, field ownership, signing, replay, authorization,
  failure, privacy, support, rollout, and rollback requirements are reviewed.
- Compatibility is documented, but portal-side implementation acceptance is
  not yet claimed.

D2 LOCAL ADAPTER IMPLEMENTED AND VERIFIED

- The portal-side adapter, import/export boundary, claim/create/manage links,
  receipts, suppression, and failure handling exist in the exact portal
  artifact at the claimed level.
- Local unit, schema, contract-fixture, replay, mismatch, failure, and rollback
  tests pass against the exact declared contract inputs.
- D2 does not prove that the exact Smarter Justice producer and portal consumer
  have completed joint local acceptance.

D3 LOCAL INTEGRATION ACCEPTED

- Producer and consumer contract acceptance is reproduced using the exact
  declared Smarter Justice and portal artifacts in a legitimate local or
  equivalent controlled integration environment.
- Field ownership, signing, replay, idempotency, receipts, suppression,
  mismatch, stale-data, last-known-good, support, and rollback behavior pass.

D4 STAGING ACCEPTED

- End-to-end staging acceptance passes with the exact declared Smarter Justice
  and portal artifacts, authentic staging configuration, monitoring, support,
  incident, backup/restore, and rollback evidence.

D5 PRODUCTION ACTIVE AND POST-DEPLOYMENT VERIFIED

- The exact bound production artifact and configuration are deployed.
- Authentic post-deployment verification passes for the identified integration
  boundary, including monitoring, support, incident response, reconciliation,
  rollback readiness, and current production-version evidence.
- The exact scoped owner decision receipt authorizes live activation for that
  product, environment, capability, and artifact.
- D5 does not exist during `DEPLOYMENT IN PROGRESS` or `DEPLOYED — POST-
  DEPLOYMENT VERIFICATION PENDING`.

INTEGRATION-MATURITY RULES

- Progress through the ladder sequentially unless the complete evidence for
  every lower state is independently reproduced and recorded.
- Do not infer D2 from a schema, route, environment variable, fixture, or code
  presence alone.
- Do not infer D3 from portal-only tests, copied fixtures, or a Smarter Justice
  handoff alone.
- Do not infer D4 from local browser or simulated network tests.
- Do not infer D5 from deployment files, DNS, a production URL, or owner intent
  without live acceptance and monitoring evidence.
- Downgrade the state when the contract, exact artifact, signing rules, field
  ownership, data schema, environment, gate, or acceptance evidence materially
  changes or regresses.
- A higher historical state does not remain current after a material change
  until the affected acceptance is rerun.
- Promotion or acceptance must name the exact `INTEGRATION_BOUNDARY_ID`; do not
  use a general product label as evidence for an untested path.
- A path may advance only when its own producer, consumer, data, failure,
  suppression, support, monitoring, and rollback evidence passes at the claimed
  environment.

CURRENTLY IN PROGRESS

- Exists in current working state but not final artifact

LAUNCH CRITICAL REMAINING

- Required before intended launch

OPTIONAL OR LATER

- Useful but not required now

BLOCKED

- Cannot be completed because of a specific external dependency or decision

REJECTED

- Explicitly not to be built

SUPERSEDED

- Historical direction replaced by a newer controlling decision

OWNER DEPENDENT

- Requires Roger's business, legal, pricing, credential, deployment, or risk decision.
- When builder-controlled prerequisites are complete, replace this broad label
  with the exact Owner Assistance Request state defined in the canonical launch
  section. A launch-critical item may not remain passively `OWNER DEPENDENT`.

SIMULATED OR FIXTURE-ONLY

- Test or example, not operational

Do not use vague labels such as “ready” without identifying the exact environment and evidence.
