45. UPDATED FINAL QUALITY-CONTROL ADDITIONS
===============================================================================

Before final delivery, also confirm:

- Every claimant must be verified before control.
- Email verification alone cannot grant control.
- Payment cannot affect verification.
- Pending claims cannot publish protected changes.
- Approved claims preserve the canonical profile URL.
- Claim, credential, specialty, publication, paid placement, and opportunity states remain separate.
- Source-based publication uses approved lawful sources.
- Initial first-publication decisions remain human approved unless a source class passed the Automatic Publication Gate.
- Every public unclaimed profile has claim, correction, suppression, and removal.
- Public language does not imply attorney participation.
- Outreach is separate from publication and verification.
- Outreach starts manual-first.
- Outreach uses approved professional contact sources.
- Opt-out and do-not-contact controls work.
- Outreach never grants control.
- Outreach never verifies identity.
- Outreach never activates paid products.
- Automatic publication and automated outreach remain closed unless their separate gates are accepted and owner approved.
- Claim verification, publication, and outreach records do not leak private evidence or contact lists.

===============================================================================
46. UNIQUE FULL INDIVIDUAL PROFESSIONAL, FIRM, PRACTICE, AND APPLICABLE ORGANIZATION PROFILE PAGE — ABSOLUTE REQUIREMENT
===============================================================================

Every approved public attorney, tax professional, or other approved individual professional must receive one unique, full, canonical profile page for that one human professional only on the assigned micro-portal. Every approved public firm, tax practice, and applicable organization/service-provider record must receive its own separate unique, complete, canonical entity page when that profile class is authorized for public publication.

This applies to:

- Seeded public unclaimed profiles
- Claimed profiles
- Verified profiles
- Profiles created after a verified central application
- Firms
- Professionals and firms imported through the approved Smarter Justice handoff
- Approved tax professionals, including attorneys, CPAs, enrolled agents, and
  other credential or qualification classes supported by current authoritative
  sources and the assigned portal
- Other approved individual professional classes registered for the portal
- Tax practices and other approved professional entities
- Approved Domestic Violence Aid advocates, legal-aid organizations, nonprofits, public agencies, shelters, and service providers imported or created through an accepted profile-class workflow

An individual professional profile page is not complete merely because:

- The professional appears in search results
- The professional appears on a firm, practice, or organization roster
- The portal renders a generic shared page
- A card, modal, drawer, or listing contains a few fields
- Smarter Justice has a central management page
- A JSON record exists
- A fixture or sample page exists

ONE-PROFESSIONAL-PER-PAGE ABSOLUTE RULE

- One canonical individual-professional page represents exactly one human
  professional.
- Never combine spouses, partners, teams, multiple attorneys, multiple tax
  professionals, or a professional and firm into one canonical individual page.
- A group photograph may appear only as clearly secondary firm or practice
  media when rights-authorized; it cannot replace the one-person identity and
  canonical-page rule.
- A professional who belongs to multiple firms, offices, or practices retains
  one canonical individual page per portal with approved relationships rather
  than duplicate person pages.
- Firm, practice, and organization pages remain separate entity pages and link
  bidirectionally to approved individual pages.
- Search cards, featured-professional modules, carousels, comparison views,
  rosters, and maps must link to the unique canonical page and may not become a
  substitute canonical profile.

A firm profile page is not complete merely because:

- The firm name appears on an attorney page
- The firm appears in search results
- The firm has only a contact card
- A generic template is not bound to a stable firm entity
- The firm has no supported office or attorney relationships

ATTORNEY PAGE MINIMUM ACCEPTANCE

Each approved public attorney page must have:

- Stable portal attorney ID
- Stable central professional ID when known
- One canonical HTTPS URL
- Entity-specific page content
- Full approved professional name
- Professional title
- Portal-specific practice evidence
- General and specialty biography when supported
- Firm and office relationships
- Link to the correct firm page when applicable
- Approved public business contact information
- Website
- Jurisdictions and current professional-status information
- Education and languages where supported
- Matter types and service geography where supported
- Claim state
- Verification state
- Source and currentness information
- Claim path
- Correction path
- Suppression or removal path
- Manage path to Smarter Justice after claim
- Search-index entry
- Canonical and sitemap handling where appropriate
- Structured data only when accurate
- Mobile, tablet, desktop, keyboard, zoom, and screen-reader acceptance
- Useful missing-field behavior
- Optional rights-authorized professional headshot and optional practice logo,
  each with accepted state, accessible alternative text, neutral fallback,
  replacement/removal controls, and no credential or endorsement implication
- Truthful Sponsored label only when separately active
- No unsupported endorsement, availability, rating, outcome, or best-match claim

TAX-PROFESSIONAL AND OTHER INDIVIDUAL-PROFESSIONAL PAGE MINIMUM ACCEPTANCE

Each approved public tax-professional or other individual-professional page must
satisfy every applicable individual-page requirement above and additionally:

- identify the exact professional class without implying that every tax
  preparer is an attorney, CPA, enrolled agent, licensed, certified, or endorsed;
- distinguish current credential or qualification, issuing authority,
  jurisdiction or federal scope, currentness, and source where supported;
- distinguish unlimited, limited, or no representation rights before the IRS
  only when current authoritative evidence supports the exact statement;
- never display a PTIN publicly unless lawful, necessary, source-supported, and
  approved; possession of a PTIN alone must not be described as an endorsement
  or broad professional credential;
- show tax services, populations, languages, availability, year-round contact,
  e-file or representation capability, and service geography only when supported;
- provide correction, opt-out, complaint-resource, and source/currentness paths;
- provide an optional professional headshot and optional practice logo under the
  same free safe-media controls and neutral-fallback rules; and
- use `Grow your tax practice` rather than `Grow your law practice` when the
  professional is not presented as a lawyer.

FIRM PAGE MINIMUM ACCEPTANCE

Each approved public firm page must have:

- Stable portal firm ID
- Stable central firm ID when known
- One canonical HTTPS URL
- Entity-specific page content
- Full approved firm name
- Firm biography when supported
- Offices
- Website and public business contact
- Portal-relevant practice areas
- Languages where supported
- Verified or review-approved attorney roster
- Links to each approved affiliated attorney's unique portal profile
- Relationship currentness
- Claim and authority state
- Source and currentness information
- Claim path
- Correction path
- Suppression or removal path
- Search-index entry
- Mobile, tablet, desktop, keyboard, zoom, and screen-reader acceptance
- Optional rights-authorized firm or practice logo with accepted state,
  accessible alternative text, neutral fallback, replacement/removal controls,
  and no endorsement implication
- Truthful Sponsored label only when separately active

ORGANIZATION/SERVICE-PROVIDER PAGE MINIMUM ACCEPTANCE — DOMESTIC VIOLENCE AID

Each approved public page must have:

- Stable portal organization/service ID.
- Stable central organization ID when the exact supported central contract provides one.
- Exact profile class and supported legal status.
- One canonical HTTPS URL.
- Entity-specific content.
- Approved name.
- Organization-controlled or authoritative source support.
- Public-safe website, phone, email, and service-area information.
- Public location only when publication is safe, authorized, and sourced.
- Confidential/nonpublic location suppression.
- Languages, populations served, accessibility, and services only when supported.
- Availability and emergency status only when current and explicitly supported.
- Source and last-checked information.
- Claimed/unclaimed and authority-review state.
- Correction, removal, urgent safety-suppression, and restoration paths.
- Search-index entry when public.
- Mobile, tablet, desktop, keyboard, zoom, and screen-reader acceptance.
- Optional rights-authorized organization logo only when safe and approved, with
  neutral fallback, alternative text, replacement/removal, and no endorsement implication.
- No unsupported endorsement, capacity, eligibility, shelter availability, government status, emergency response, outcome, or quality claim.
- Stop Sign Project relationship only when supported and approved.

RELATIONSHIP AND CANONICAL ACCEPTANCE

- Attorney-to-firm and firm-to-attorney links must be bidirectional where approved.
- Former, disputed, ambiguous, or unsupported relationships must not be shown as current.
- Multi-office and multi-firm relationships require explicit rules.
- A firm administrator cannot silently assign attorneys or overwrite protected attorney facts.
- Profile merges and splits must preserve stable identity, redirects, audit, and rollback.
- The same attorney, tax professional, other individual professional, firm, practice, organization, or service provider must not receive multiple canonical pages in the same portal merely because records came from different sources.
- Every build must test canonical uniqueness across the full current dataset, including organization/service-provider entities where applicable.

===============================================================================
46A. COMPETITOR-BENCHMARKED PROFILE QUALITY, SAFE PROFESSIONAL MEDIA, AND PRACTICE-GROWTH ENTRY POINT
===============================================================================

CURRENT OFFICIAL PROFILE-QUALITY RESEARCH — REQUIRED

Before a material profile-system build, major profile redesign, or stale-portal
profile reactivation:

1. Research at least three current relevant official competitor, directory, or
   authoritative professional-profile experiences. For attorney portals,
   normally include current official material from services such as Avvo,
   Justia, Lawyers.com/Martindale, FindLaw, or Super Lawyers when accessible and
   relevant. For tax-professional experiences, include current IRS credential,
   qualification, directory, and representation-right guidance plus relevant
   professional-directory experiences.
2. Record source, access date, exact feature observed, target user, evidence
   quality, legal/design reuse limits, and whether the finding remains current.
3. Compare representative search, individual profile, firm/practice profile,
   claim, edit, contact, mobile, accessibility, and professional-growth paths.
4. Preserve screenshots or other allowed evidence only when lawful and useful;
   do not package copyrighted competitor assets unnecessarily.
5. Do not copy protected text, images, layout, trade dress, ratings, reviews,
   badges, ranking methods, or proprietary data.

PROFILE COMPETITIVE-QUALITY MATRIX

Benchmark at least:

- one-human-professional canonical-page uniqueness;
- fast first impression, clear identity, specialty, location, and contact path;
- professional headshot and practice/firm logo control;
- biography, experience, practice/service areas, credentials, jurisdictions,
  languages, offices, firm/practice relationships, and supported availability;
- claim, verification, free correction, removal, opt-out, and update paths;
- source, last-checked, claim, verification, membership, and sponsored-state
  transparency;
- reviews, awards, ratings, disciplinary, result, and recognition handling only
  when lawfully sourced, current, contextualized, and not misleading;
- mobile, keyboard, screen-reader, zoom, contrast, performance, and structured-
  data quality;
- public-user connection to useful official resources, Matter Path, Document
  Help, preparation tools, non-AI fallback, and privacy/safety controls; and
- professional onboarding, firm/practice management, covered-seat, support,
  and truthful membership-value paths.

The acceptance target is not visual imitation. Match the useful core directory
capabilities that current users and professionals reasonably expect, then prove
materially better treatment where justified through clearer source/currentness
truth, specialty depth, credential boundaries, accessible design, privacy,
correction/removal, free control, connected public-help value, and honest state
separation. Keep internal benchmark conclusions in evidence. Do not publish a
public `better than Avvo`, `best`, `top`, or similar comparative claim without
separate current legal, evidence, and owner approval.

SAFE PROFESSIONAL MEDIA CONTROL

Support optional claimant-controlled media through the approved Smarter Justice
or portal contract without creating divergent authority:

- Individual professional: professional headshot/portrait and optional practice
  logo. Prefer the headshot as primary identity media when supplied; a practice
  logo may be secondary or may fill the primary visual slot when no headshot is
  supplied, but the page must remain person-centric and clearly labeled.
- Firm, tax practice, or organization: entity logo controlled only by the
  verified entity administrator or approved authority.
- Free control: initial upload, crop, preview, replace, remove, and neutral
  fallback are basic factual profile controls and are not paywalled.
- Accepted inputs: use a narrow approved raster set such as JPEG, PNG, or WebP;
  safely sanitize and rasterize any approved vector-logo workflow rather than
  serving untrusted raw SVG or active content.
- Validation: verify magic bytes, dimensions, pixel count, file size, animation
  policy, decompression limits, corruption, malware, and content-type mismatch;
  reject executables, polyglots, scripts, trackers, and remote hotlinks.
- Privacy: strip unnecessary EXIF, GPS, device, and author metadata; do not run
  face recognition, biometric matching, emotion analysis, or protected-trait
  inference.
- Processing: re-encode, orient, crop with user-controlled focal point, generate
  bounded responsive variants, preserve aspect ratio, and prevent image bombs.
- Rights and truth: require an attestation that the claimant owns or may use the
  media and has consent from depicted people; prohibit deceptive impersonation,
  fake credentials, unauthorized third-party branding, confidential material,
  and synthetic photorealistic portraits presented as the real professional.
- Moderation: first publication and materially changed identity media require
  the applicable automated and human review; record pending, accepted, rejected,
  reason, reviewer, appeal/correction, and revalidation state without exposing
  private evidence.
- Accessibility: provide useful claimant-supplied or reviewed alternative text,
  use empty alt text for purely decorative duplicates, never place essential
  credential text only inside an image, and provide a neutral initials or entity
  fallback.
- Lifecycle: version, audit, rollback, replace, remove, propagate suppression,
  purge cached and derivative copies, and prevent stale restoration.
- Safety: Domestic Violence Aid and other sensitive portals apply stricter
  identity, location, survivor-safety, organization-address, and threat-model
  controls; a public image is always optional.

PROFESSIONAL-GROWTH ENTRY POINT

Use these preferred primary labels:

- Attorney and law-firm surfaces: `Grow your law practice`.
- Tax-professional and tax-practice surfaces: `Grow your tax practice`.

Avoid `Grow your legal practice`, which is less natural, and avoid the ambiguous
standalone `Grow your practice` as the primary label unless the surrounding
context makes the professional type unmistakable and a shorter responsive label
is genuinely necessary. The accessible name must retain the full specialty-
specific meaning.

Recommended supporting value proposition:

`Claim and complete your profile, present supported experience and credentials,
manage your firm or practice, and help people understand whether your services
may fit their needs.`

Adapt the wording to the specialty and actual capabilities. Keep it factual.
Never promise leads, clients, matters, revenue, ranking, exclusivity,
representation, or outcomes. Explain free factual profile control before paid
membership, display the current centrally approved pricing only when available,
and provide direct paths to claim, create, manage, compare plans, contact
support, correct information, and opt out.

PROFILE VISUAL AND HUMAN ACCEPTANCE

Test at representative mobile, tablet, laptop, and large-desktop widths. Confirm:

- one clear professional identity above the fold without overcrowding;
- headshot/logo crops remain respectful, sharp, and non-distorting;
- placeholder states look intentional rather than broken;
- name, professional class, specialty, location, claim/verification state, and
  primary contact or next step remain understandable without relying on image;
- the professional-growth label is visible, specific, keyboard accessible, and
  understandable in navigation and on the landing page;
- no profile page resembles a shared roster masquerading as an individual page;
- no false review, rating, award, endorsement, availability, or best-match cue;
  and
- representative attorneys, tax professionals, firms, and public users complete
  the profile, claim, media, correction, and contact paths successfully.

Create a Profile Competitive-Quality Record, Canonical Uniqueness Record,
Professional Media Safety Record, and Practice-Growth Entry-Point Record. A
screenshot alone is not acceptance; bind evidence to the exact artifact,
route, viewport, data fixture or accepted record, state, and test time.

===============================================================================
46B. CROSS-PORTAL PROFESSIONAL IDENTITY, PUBLISHABLE PROFILE UTILITY, AND REVIEW/RATING/TESTIMONIAL TRUTH
===============================================================================

ONE HUMAN, ONE NETWORK IDENTITY, PORTAL-SPECIFIC PAGES

For every accepted individual professional:

- maintain one central Smarter Justice professional identity and approved stable
  external identifiers where lawfully available;
- create no more than one person-specific canonical public page in the assigned
  portal;
- bind every portal page to the central identity without turning the central
  record into a substitute public portal page or automatically exposing a public
  cross-portal dossier;
- require portal-specific specialty, jurisdiction, geography, publication,
  currentness, and safety evidence before that portal page is accepted;
- do not infer that acceptance in one portal proves acceptance in another;
- do not create another subscription, paid seat, claim authority, or billing
  relationship because another portal page exists; and
- keep central-owned and portal-owned fields explicit, versioned, auditable, and
  conflict-aware; and
- publish cross-portal links only when relevant, authorized, and safe for the
  professional and the portal specialty.

ENTITY RESOLUTION, DUPLICATES, MERGE, AND RECOVERY

Name similarity alone is insufficient. Use approved stable IDs, corroborating
identity facts, professional-controlled evidence, and source lineage. Classify
matches as `MATCHED`, `POSSIBLE MATCH — REVIEW REQUIRED`, `CONFLICTING MATCH —
QUARANTINED`, `DISTINCT PEOPLE`, `MERGED WITH REDIRECT`, or `UNMERGED/RESTORED`.

A merge must preserve:

- every source and field-level provenance record;
- canonical URL history and safe redirects;
- claim, authority, membership, seat, suppression, removal, media, firm, office,
  specialty, jurisdiction, and opportunity states without unauthorized widening;
- audit history and exact rollback; and
- propagation to search, caches, exports, sitemaps, analytics identifiers, and
  every applicable portal projection without stale restoration.

A disagreement between central and portal data does not authorize silent last-
write-wins behavior. Apply field ownership, quarantine the affected value, retain
the last accepted safe public state when appropriate, issue a correction task,
and keep unrelated profile functions available when safe.

PUBLISHABLE PROFILE UTILITY ACCEPTANCE

A counted accepted public profile must be more useful than a directory stub. In
addition to the existing quality floor, verify on the rendered canonical page:

- clear individual or entity identity and professional class;
- supported portal specialty or service relevance;
- jurisdiction, service geography, or office context where material;
- truthful credential, registration, license, and status boundaries appropriate
  to the professional class;
- a safe useful public contact, inquiry, or next-step path, or a clearly explained
  evidence-based unavailable state;
- current source/review dates and understandable claim, verification, membership,
  sponsored, and currentness labels;
- correction, suppression, removal, privacy, and support paths;
- accessible mobile-ready structure, stable canonical URL, and search integration;
- supported person-to-firm/practice/organization relationships; and
- enough meaningful public information to help a reasonable user assess possible
  relevance without implying quality, endorsement, availability, representation,
  or result.

Sensitive portals may intentionally publish fewer fields. Record the safety
reason and preserve usefulness through safe alternatives; never pad the profile
with invented biography, contact, specialty, credential, review, or availability
content.

REVIEWS, RATINGS, TESTIMONIALS, ENDORSEMENTS, AND FEATURED PLACEMENT

Default state: no star rating, aggregate score, review count, testimonial carousel,
`recommended`, `best match`, award, badge, or featured-professional cue unless the
exact feature has passed its own rights, provenance, policy, moderation,
accessibility, security, privacy, legal, owner, calculation, dispute, correction,
removal, and rollback acceptance.

Never:

- copy or import a competitor's review, rating, count, badge, award, or testimonial;
- fabricate social proof, use fixtures publicly, or imply reviews exist when they
  do not;
- suppress ordinary negative feedback merely because a professional pays;
- exchange undisclosed compensation, discounts, priority, or benefits for a
  favorable review or testimonial;
- use case outcomes, confidential client facts, or unverifiable claims as profile
  quality proof;
- calculate an opaque score or rank that cannot be reproduced and explained; or
- let sponsored or featured placement alter organic profile truth, credential
  state, claim state, or opportunity eligibility.

When an approved featured placement exists, label it plainly as `Sponsored`,
`Paid placement`, `Editorially featured`, or another exact truthful state.
`Featured attorney` without the governing basis is prohibited.

REQUIRED ACCEPTANCE EVIDENCE

Create:

1. a Network Professional Identity and Portal-Page Binding Record;
2. a Duplicate, Merge, Redirect, Unmerge, and Stale-Restoration Test Record;
3. a Publishable Profile Utility and Growth-Counting Record; and
4. a Reviews, Ratings, Testimonials, Endorsements, and Featured-Placement Truth
   Record.

Bind each record to the exact artifact, data state, portal, central contract,
representative pages, tests, dates, owner/legal decisions, conflicts, rollback,
and continuation.

===============================================================================
47. PAID-MONTHLY-MEMBER ATTORNEY-REVIEW AND LEGAL-HELP OPPORTUNITY ALLOCATION
===============================================================================

This section applies only to attorneys and covered firm seats. It does not make advocates, nonprofits, agencies, shelters, service providers, community organizations, or The Stop Sign Project eligible to receive legal matters, legal fees, or attorney-review opportunities.

The platform must have a real, auditable system for assigning public-user requests for attorney review or legal help.

The system must not rely on:

- Unmanaged email forwarding
- A hidden spreadsheet without authoritative records
- Developer edits
- Random assignments without audit
- Payment return pages
- Highest payer
- Public profile rank
- Unverified attorneys
- Attorneys without current portal eligibility
- Attorneys whose required paid monthly profile membership is inactive

CONTROLLING ELIGIBILITY RULE

Before any attorney-review request, legal-help request, lead, case opportunity, or referral opportunity is offered, assigned, delivered, or made visible:

- The attorney must already have an active paid monthly profile membership recorded centrally in Smarter Justice before the allocation event.
- A firm-covered attorney must occupy an active paid covered seat.
- Payment must be confirmed through server-authoritative central billing and entitlement state.
- A browser success page, checkout start, pending invoice, failed payment, past-due membership, cancelled membership, disputed membership, fixture, or simulation does not qualify.
- Annual or alternative plans qualify only if Roger later explicitly approves them as equivalent for opportunity eligibility.
- Free claiming, verification, correction, approved basic editing, suppression, and removal remain available without paid membership.

ACTIVE PAYMENT IS REQUIRED BUT IS NOT SUFFICIENT

Each opportunity recipient must also have:

- Verified central account
- Verified claimant identity
- Verified current professional status
- Approved claim authority
- Approved portal specialty evidence
- Approved portal participation
- Relevant jurisdiction
- Relevant geography
- Current opportunity terms
- Conflicts-process readiness
- Privacy and data-handling acceptance
- Current delivery channel
- Capacity or availability state where supported
- No suspension, revocation, death, impersonation, fraud, or serious dispute flag
- No owner or staff hold
- Active opportunity-access gate
- Applicable legal and owner approvals

The system must distinguish:

- Free public profile
- Claimed profile
- Verified profile
- Paid monthly membership
- Sponsored placement
- Organic ordering
- Opportunity eligibility
- Opportunity allocation
- Attorney acceptance
- Representation
- Legal outcome

None automatically creates another.

PUBLIC USER REQUEST

A request should:

1. Identify portal and matter category.
2. Identify jurisdiction and relevant geography.
3. Collect only minimum necessary information.
4. Present safe urgency or deadline guidance where appropriate.
5. Obtain informed consent for routing.
6. State that submission does not create an attorney-client relationship.
7. State that response, acceptance, representation, and outcome are not guaranteed.
8. Capture contact preferences.
9. Allow withdrawal where appropriate.
10. Create a private opportunity record.
11. Avoid releasing unnecessary confidential detail before preliminary conflict and acceptance steps.

ELIGIBLE POOL

The system must build the eligible pool from current server-authoritative records.

Exclude attorneys or firm seats that fail:

- Active paid monthly profile membership
- Verification
- Specialty
- Jurisdiction
- Geography
- Terms
- Conflict readiness
- Capacity
- Support
- Security
- Current contact
- Opportunity gate
- Suppression or hold rules

ALLOCATION POLICY

The initial allocation policy must be documented, fair, deterministic, auditable, and owner approved.

Relevant noncommercial factors may include:

- Specialty fit
- Matter-type fit
- Jurisdiction
- Geography
- Language requested
- User preference
- Capacity
- Conflict availability
- Fair rotation
- Time since last eligible opportunity
- Operational response history used carefully and truthfully
- Stable tie-breaker

Payment establishes threshold eligibility. It must not secretly buy higher organic rank or allow the highest payer to monopolize requests.

Possible owner-approved modes:

- Sequential fair rotation
- Limited simultaneous offer to a small qualified pool
- Public-user selection from an eligible pool
- Staff-assisted assignment for exceptional matters
- Verified firm-seat routing

OFFER, ACCEPTANCE, AND REASSIGNMENT

Support:

- Opportunity ID
- Offer ID
- Recipient
- Sent time
- Delivery state
- Response deadline
- Viewed
- Conflict decline
- Capacity decline
- Accepted for preliminary contact
- Expired
- Withdrawn
- Reassigned
- User cancelled
- Staff hold
- Support state

Attorney acceptance does not automatically create representation.

Before detailed confidential information is released, support an appropriate preliminary conflict and engagement step.

If an attorney declines, expires, becomes ineligible, or cannot be reached:

- Reassign according to the approved policy.
- Preserve complete audit.
- Do not expose the request to unlimited recipients.
- Inform the public user truthfully.
- Stop after withdrawal or the approved routing limit.

MEMBERSHIP FAILURE

When required paid monthly membership becomes inactive:

- Stop new opportunity delivery.
- Preserve free profile-control rights.
- Do not automatically remove the basic public profile.
- Preserve audit for existing accepted opportunities.
- Reconcile membership, seat, entitlement, and portal state.
- Handle already accepted matters lawfully and truthfully.

PROHIBITED FEE AND CLAIM STRUCTURES

- Percentage of legal fees
- Percentage of settlement, recovery, refund, award, or result
- Retention-contingent charges
- Result-contingent charges
- Nonlawyer fee sharing
- Disguised referral fees
- Guaranteed referrals
- Guaranteed response
- Guaranteed representation
- Guaranteed revenue
- Guaranteed outcome

OWNER AND STAFF OPERATIONS

Authorized staff must be able to:

- View requests
- View eligibility reasons
- View eligible pool
- See exclusion reasons
- Approve or pause routing
- Review allocation
- Reassign
- Handle conflict and capacity declines
- Handle delivery failure
- Handle withdrawal
- Handle complaints
- Suspend a professional
- Reconcile membership
- Audit every offer and decision
- Use an emergency kill switch

No routine assignment should require editing source files or JSON.

REQUIRED TESTS

- Active paid monthly member
- Unpaid member
- Cancelled member
- Past-due member
- Disputed payment
- Unverified claimant
- Unsupported specialty
- Wrong jurisdiction
- Wrong geography
- No capacity
- Conflict decline
- Covered firm seat
- Uncovered firm attorney
- Sponsored but not opportunity eligible
- Opportunity eligible but not Sponsored
- Fair rotation
- Stable tie-breaker
- User-selected attorney
- Expiration and reassignment
- Delivery failure
- User withdrawal
- Membership lapse after acceptance
- No detailed confidential release before required acceptance
- No automatic attorney-client relationship
- Complete audit
- Gate closed
- Kill switch

Do not call this operational until payment, legal, privacy, support, allocation, staging, and owner gates pass.

===============================================================================
48. CONTINUAL EVALUATION AND EVIDENCE-BASED IMPROVEMENT OF PUBLIC TOOLS
===============================================================================

Maintain every public-user tool in the portal's current Tool Inventory and
Maturity Map.

During every material build:

- run a portal-wide existence, route, broken-state, public-claim, and dependency
  regression scan;
- deeply evaluate tools changed by the release, affected by shared code or
  data, shown in the attorney-demonstration path, carrying stale or
  time-sensitive content, handling sensitive information, previously failing,
  or selected in the Release Scope Record;
- perform the full tool-by-tool evaluation during a Full Milestone Release.

This includes:

- Issue selectors
- Pathways
- Readiness tools
- Checklists
- Organizers
- Calculators when legally appropriate
- Timelines
- Basic forms
- Document generators
- Upload and review tools
- Search
- Profile comparison
- Official-resource navigation
- Contact and attorney-review pathways
- Saved work
- Download, print, and export
- Empty, loading, error, success, restart, and recovery states

For every deeply reviewed tool, record:

- Intended user
- User problem
- Current maturity
- Inputs
- Outputs
- Legal and jurisdiction boundaries
- Source freshness
- Privacy
- Accessibility
- Mobile behavior
- Performance
- Support
- Known friction
- Evidence for change
- Recommended disposition

Permitted dispositions:

- Improve now
- Preserve unchanged because it works
- Simplify
- Repair
- Hide until complete
- Replace
- Merge
- Defer
- Remove from launch
- Reject

CONTROLLING CHANGE RULE

Improve a tool when a genuine, evidence-based, testable benefit is reasonably achievable.

Do not change a tool:

- Merely for activity
- Merely to change appearance
- Merely to increase version number
- Merely because another portal differs
- When existing behavior is clearer, safer, or more reliable
- Without preserving saved work and migration compatibility
- Without testing the exact final artifact

Every material build should implement the highest-priority connected tool
improvements selected in the Release Scope Record. Preserve the remaining
high-value opportunities in the Next Version Improvement List with evidence,
priority, dependencies, and acceptance criteria.

When preserving a tool unchanged, record why preservation is the better
decision.

===============================================================================
48A. COHERENT DOCUMENT HELP CENTER AND SEPARATE USER ENTRY POINTS
===============================================================================

Each portal must provide or progressively build a clear Document Help entry area appropriate to its legal specialty. This is an information architecture and workflow standard, not an instruction to force every function into one code module or public screen.

The default umbrella may use separate user entry points for:

1. Draft a letter or notice.
2. Upload and review a document.
3. Compare two or more documents or versions.
4. Receive analysis, feedback, issue identification, and suggested next steps.
5. Prepare or review an agreement or portal-appropriate draft.
6. Find a current official court or government form.
7. Complete guided preparation for an official form.
8. Use a form-filling workspace when safely supported.
9. Organize documents, facts, and questions for an attorney.
10. Request access to an advanced form builder when that controlled capability exists.

Do not show every entry point merely for visual completeness. The primary
public Document Help area and attorney-demonstration path should show only
operational capabilities or a necessary safely gated action with a useful
explanation and next step. Planned, unavailable, blocked, or owner-dependent
capabilities belong in internal maturity records or a separate clearly
identified roadmap—not as prominent public tool cards that make the portal look
unfinished.

Do not send a user into a dead end or use one generic upload box for unrelated
purposes without explaining the workflow.

REQUIRED CAPABILITY DISTINCTIONS

The public experience must distinguish:

- OFFICIAL FORM LIBRARY: discovery of the current official form and authoritative source.
- GUIDED OFFICIAL-FORM PREPARATION: structured assistance that maps user answers to an official form or preparation packet without claiming filing or legal representation.
- FORM-FILLING WORKSPACE: safe completion, review, save, export, and deletion behavior.
- PORTAL-CREATED DRAFT: a versioned draft letter, notice, agreement, organizer, or preparation document clearly labeled as nonofficial and subject to its activation gate.
- DOCUMENT REVIEW: private review, extraction, organization, comparison, issue spotting, and suggested next steps.
- ATTORNEY OR HUMAN REVIEW PREPARATION: organizing selected information for a consented review request.
- ELECTRONIC FILING: prohibited from being represented as available unless authorized, integrated, tested, supported, and explicitly approved.

OFFICIAL-FORM CURRENTNESS

Official forms must come from current authoritative sources such as courts, the IRS, the U.S. Department of State, other federal agencies, state agencies, or city agencies. Preserve form title, form number, revision or edition date, jurisdiction, source URL or identifier, retrieval date, effective or expiration information when available, checksum when practical, and last revalidation date.

Never present an old official form as current. When currentness cannot be verified, keep the form unavailable or clearly mark it as requiring verification. A portal-created imitation must not be presented as the official form.

SUPPORTED INPUT AND FILE-TYPE TRUTH

Public copy may name only file types and sizes the exact portal actually accepts and safely processes. PDF, images, screenshots, office documents, plain text, email exports, and other formats require format-specific validation, malware protection, extraction behavior, error handling, retention, deletion, and regression evidence. Do not use vague "other formats" language to imply universal support.

PORTAL-SPECIFIC FIT

- Divorce Law Aid should prioritize agreements, court and financial documents, parenting and support materials, official family-court forms, letters, notices, and attorney-preparation records.
- Estate Law Aid should prioritize wills, trusts, powers of attorney, directives, deeds, probate and administration forms, fiduciary records, letters, notices, and attorney-preparation records.
- Personal Injury Law Aid should prioritize incident, treatment, expense, wage-loss, insurance, evidence, preservation, records-request, and attorney-preparation materials, excluding workers' compensation and medical-malpractice scope.
- Domestic Violence Aid should prioritize safety-conscious organizers, source maps, court and agency preparation, selected official forms, safe-contact choices, evidence indexes, and attorney-preparation materials. Shared-device, browser-history, notification, storage, retaliation, and confidential-location risks control every document workflow.

DOCUMENT HELP CENTER ACCEPTANCE

The center is accepted only when:

- labels and distinctions are understandable without legal or technical expertise;
- the entry points reflect actual maturity and route to real workflows;
- no incomplete tool is presented as operational;
- jurisdiction and official-source boundaries are visible;
- privacy, storage, retention, deletion, export, and human-review behavior are clear;
- mobile, keyboard, screen-reader, zoom, error, empty, loading, and recovery states are accepted;
- the user can understand what happened, what remains uncertain, and what to do next;
- the exact final artifact contains tests for representative routes and failure cases.

===============================================================================
48B. SHARED MATTER PATH, READINESS, EXPLAINABILITY, NON-AI FALLBACK, AND PAID-AI-PROVIDER ACTIVATION
===============================================================================

Review the newest exact Smarter Justice Matter Path learning on every material
portal build. Adopt or adapt the verified beneficial principle when it fits the
assigned specialty, without copying central-only logic, erasing portal identity,
or presenting a central routing result as portal specialty truth.

SPECIALTY-ADAPTED MATTER PATH STANDARD

Where relevant and mature, preserve or progressively build a portal-owned
starting journey that can:

- identify the user's selected issue and specialty path;
- identify the matter stage without pretending to make a legal determination;
- identify missing factual information and missing documents;
- explain what information affected the path and what remains uncertain;
- produce an explainable preparation or readiness score whose inputs,
  limitations, and nonlegal meaning are visible;
- ask one question at a time where that improves completion while preserving
  back navigation, correction, answer history, save/resume where accepted,
  export, and deletion;
- show the public user current status, missing items, suggested next actions,
  uncertainty, support, and recovery;
- provide authorized staff a reviewable `why this path was chosen` record,
  relevant source/evidence inputs, missing-information state, override reason,
  and audit receipt;
- hand off only the minimum approved specialty-safe data to Smarter Justice or
  another portal through the exact accepted contract; and
- prevent silent path changes, unsupported legal conclusions, guaranteed
  deadlines, guaranteed outcomes, filing claims, representation claims, or
  false attorney review.

PORTAL-SPECIFIC ADAPTATION

- Divorce Law Aid should adapt stage, missing-information, document, and
  readiness logic to divorce, separation, custody/parenting, support, property,
  financial, agreement, and approved related family-law starting paths.
- Estate Law Aid should adapt it to planning, will/trust, power-of-attorney,
  directive, probate, administration, guardianship, fiduciary, property, and
  execution-readiness paths while never treating a generated draft as signed,
  witnessed, notarized, valid, or legally effective.
- Personal Injury Law Aid should adapt it to incident, liability, treatment,
  insurance, expense, wage-loss, evidence-preservation, limitations-warning,
  and attorney-preparation paths while routing workers' compensation and
  medical malpractice to their separate portals where appropriate.
- Domestic Violence Aid must apply the survivor-safety overlay. Do not require
  precise location, unsafe contact information, detailed narratives, silent
  saving, unsafe notifications, or sensitive cross-portal transfer merely to
  calculate a path or score. Emergency and nonemergency boundaries, quick-exit
  limits, safe-device guidance, confidential-location suppression, deliberate
  sharing, and safe fallback control.

AI ASSISTANCE BOUNDARY

AI may assist with organization, plain-language explanation, question flows,
missing-information checks, source tracking, comparison, drafting support,
summaries, and suggested questions or next steps. AI must not be presented as:

- autonomous legal, tax, accounting, medical, financial, or other professional
  judgment;
- an attorney or attorney review;
- court or agency acceptance;
- filing, signature, execution, witnessing, notarization, or legal validity;
- guaranteed accuracy, deadline, availability, response, representation, or
  outcome; or
- authority to override current official sources, portal safeguards, staff
  review, owner gates, or user choices.

A safe Tier A public launch and attorney demonstration must not depend on an
external AI provider. Preserve useful deterministic or non-AI fallbacks for
issue routing, official resources, profile search, Document Help entry,
organizers, checklists, preparation, status, and recovery. If AI is unavailable,
times out, reaches quota, refuses, produces malformed output, or fails safety
checks, the portal must fail safely and preserve the user's work and a useful
next step.

PAID AI PROVIDER ACTIVATION AND ROGER ASSISTANCE

Before asking Roger to connect a paid AI vendor or key, the builder must
complete all builder-controlled work and prepare one exact Owner Assistance
Request containing:

- the selected capability, portal, environment, model/provider purpose, and
  launch tier;
- the non-AI fallback and reason the paid provider materially improves the
  selected capability;
- the exact nonsecret environment-variable or secret-reference names;
- least-privilege scope, allowed domains/endpoints, model/version controls,
  quotas, budgets, timeouts, retries, circuit breakers, and kill switch;
- the exact data sent, minimized, redacted, retained, logged, trained on,
  shared, and deleted;
- vendor terms, privacy, security, source-rights, incident, support, and
  deletion review;
- untrusted-content isolation so uploaded text, OCR, links, metadata, or
  embedded instructions cannot alter system authority, invoke tools, fetch
  arbitrary resources, expose secrets, or cross user boundaries;
- representative sandbox tests, adversarial tests, refusal and error behavior,
  monitoring, human review, rollback, and revalidation triggers; and
- a precise instruction for Roger to place the secret only in the approved
  secure store and return only a nonsecret confirmation.

A connected key proves only that a secret reference exists. It does not prove
AI acceptance, D4, D5, deployment, or live operation. Use test/sandbox mode
first. Production activation requires exact environment acceptance, scoped
owner approval, deployment, post-deployment verification, and continued-live-
operation acceptance.

MATTER PATH AND AI ACCEPTANCE

Test the exact final artifact for representative successful, incomplete,
ambiguous, corrected, resumed, deleted, unsupported, cross-portal, provider-
failure, timeout, quota, malformed-output, unsafe-content, privacy, accessibility,
mobile, and rollback states. Record whether the capability is deterministic,
AI-assisted, locally accepted, D4 accepted, deployed, post-deployment verified,
live accepted, gated, degraded, paused, or deferred. Do not collapse these
states.

===============================================================================
48C. MEMORY-FIRST DOCUMENT QUESTIONS, UNKNOWNS, AND ATTORNEY-PREPARATION EXPORT
===============================================================================

Every portal must evaluate and, where appropriate, provide a privacy-minimized
memory-first path that lets a user capture the questions, uncertainties,
missing facts, missing documents, dates to verify, and issues to discuss before
requiring a detailed sensitive narrative or a document upload.

PURPOSE AND BOUNDARY

The capability should help a user remember what matters, prepare for an
attorney or qualified professional, and move between Document Help, Matter Path,
forms, and a review package without pretending to answer unresolved legal
questions.

It must:

- work through a complete deterministic non-AI path;
- permit a useful first session with no account and no confidential upload when
  the portal architecture safely supports device-local use;
- clearly distinguish a user question, user-stated fact, inferred issue,
  missing item, official-source reference, portal suggestion, and attorney-
  review question;
- avoid converting uncertainty into a legal conclusion, deadline, filing
  instruction, evidence claim, or completed fact;
- let users review, edit, reorder, remove, and deliberately select what to
  export or share;
- use explicit save, export, upload, and share actions rather than silent
  persistence or transmission;
- state device-only, browser storage, account storage, transmission, retention,
  deletion, and recovery behavior truthfully;
- support a plain, accessible export suitable for an attorney-preparation or
  document-review package; and
- preserve provenance showing whether an item came from the user, a portal
  prompt, an official source, a deterministic rule, an AI-assisted suggestion,
  or a human reviewer.

MEMORY-FIRST DOES NOT MEAN HIDDEN MEMORY

Do not claim that the portal remembers information unless the exact storage
mechanism, scope, duration, access controls, deletion path, and recovery limits
support that statement. Do not silently retain sensitive questions, document
names, matter labels, or contact preferences across sessions. When browser-
local storage is used, provide a clear-device-data action and explain that
clearing portal data does not erase browser history, downloads, backups, or
copies already shared.

SPECIALTY ADAPTATION

- Divorce Law Aid may help organize questions about separation, children,
  support, finances, property, agreements, court papers, and documents to
  locate without asking the portal to decide contested facts.
- Estate Law Aid may help organize goals, family and beneficiary questions,
  assets, fiduciary choices, execution questions, existing documents, and
  questions for counsel without claiming a draft is executed or legally
  effective.
- Personal Injury Law Aid may help organize event chronology, treatment,
  insurance, evidence, witnesses, expenses, communications, deadlines to ask
  about, and documents to preserve without assigning fault or valuing a claim.
- Domestic Violence Aid must use the strictest safe-device, neutral-label,
  deliberate-save, deliberate-share, confidential-location, safe-contact, URL,
  referrer, analytics, download-name, and quick-exit controls. It must allow a
  minimal or no-save path and must never require a detailed survivor narrative
  to create a useful question list.

AI-ASSISTED ENHANCEMENT

An accepted AI service may suggest additional questions or classify existing
items only after the Tier E and paid-AI-provider gates pass. AI output remains
untrusted, reviewable, removable, provenance-labeled, and optional. The
complete non-AI workflow and export must remain available when AI is absent,
paused, degraded, declined, or rolled back.

ACCEPTANCE

Test empty, partial, long, duplicate, conflicting, sensitive, unsafe, offline,
storage-disabled, export, deletion, resumption, accessibility, mobile,
print/download, and rollback states. Verify that no item is silently shared,
that provenance survives export, that deleted local data does not reappear
through stale restoration, and that an exported question package cannot be
misrepresented as legal advice, attorney review, evidence, filing, or a final
legal document.

===============================================================================
49. REUSABLE GUIDED-FORMS SYSTEM
===============================================================================

Every portal must have or progressively build a reusable guided-forms system appropriate to its specialty.

The system should support public users completing basic forms and structured preparation materials without hand-coding every form.

Possible form types:

- Guided intake questionnaire
- Matter summary
- Attorney consultation preparation form
- Contact form
- Timeline
- Asset, debt, expense, damage, or financial organizer
- Party and contact list
- Witness list
- Document inventory
- Communication log
- Evidence log
- Request or preservation letter
- Information request
- Checklist
- Court or agency preparation worksheet
- Parenting information organizer
- Executor, trustee, beneficiary, or fiduciary organizer
- Incident and treatment organizer
- Insurance communication organizer
- Attorney-review request

FORM ENGINE REQUIREMENTS

- Versioned form definitions
- Stable form and field IDs
- Conditional logic
- Required and optional fields
- Plain-language instructions
- Jurisdiction and portal metadata
- Validation
- Repeatable sections
- Add, edit, remove, and reorder where appropriate
- Save and resume
- Device-only or secure-server storage classification
- Draft preview
- Print
- Accessible download or export
- Deletion
- Migration
- Error recovery
- Mobile
- Keyboard
- Screen-reader preparation
- Zoom and reflow
- Source and revision date
- Clear draft and limitation state

FORM GOVERNANCE

Every form defines:

- Purpose
- Intended user
- Jurisdiction
- Source or review authority
- Version
- Effective date
- Review date
- Data classification
- Retention
- Required disclaimers
- Output
- Official, unofficial, preparatory, or attorney-review-only status
- Submission status
- Signature or notarization needs
- Attorney-review recommendation
- Owner and legal approval
- Launch gate

Do not represent a portal-created form as an official court or government form unless it is the exact current official form with source and version preserved.

Completing a form must not be represented as:

- Filing a case
- Creating representation
- Meeting every legal requirement
- Producing a safe-to-sign document
- Producing an enforceable agreement
- Guaranteeing court acceptance
- Replacing legal advice

===============================================================================
50. JURISDICTION-CONTROLLED DRAFT DOCUMENT CREATION
===============================================================================

Public users should be able to create appropriate draft documents where the portal specialty, supported jurisdiction, authoritative sources, testing, privacy, legal review, support, and owner approval permit.

This is a required product capability to build and mature. Public activation remains fail closed until each document class passes its own gate.

“Create” means:

- Complete a guided interview
- Generate a versioned draft
- Review a clear summary
- Identify missing or uncertain information
- Download or print when approved
- Request attorney review
- Receive jurisdiction-appropriate execution or next-step information

“Create” does not mean:

- Legally valid
- Enforceable
- Filed
- Signed
- Notarized
- Trust funded
- Assets transferred
- Court accepted
- Attorney approved
- Representation created

ESTATE LAW AID

Build and mature supported workflows for:

- Basic will drafts
- Supported trust drafts, such as an approved revocable living trust workflow
- Related approved schedules or supporting drafts
- Executor and successor information
- Beneficiary provisions
- Guardian nominations
- Fiduciary powers
- Residue and specific gifts
- Digital-asset instructions where supported
- Pet-care information where supported
- Trust parties and successor trustees
- Trust property schedule or funding organizer
- Execution instructions
- Attorney-review request

Do not imply:

- A will is valid without proper execution
- A trust is funded merely because a draft exists
- A trust avoids probate in all circumstances
- Tax savings
- Medicaid eligibility
- Asset protection
- Creditor protection
- Court acceptance

Route complex trusts, tax planning, special-needs trusts, irrevocable trusts, business succession, international matters, contested capacity, undue influence, and comparable high-risk matters to qualified attorney review unless a separately approved workflow exists.

DIVORCE LAW AID

Build and mature supported workflows for:

- Prenuptial agreement drafts
- Postnuptial agreement drafts
- Financial disclosure organizer
- Asset and debt schedule
- Separate and marital property organizer
- Income and support-information organizer
- Business-interest organizer
- Residence and real-property organizer
- Counsel and review acknowledgments where appropriate
- Execution and notarization information where appropriate
- Attorney-review request

Account for:

- Jurisdiction
- Timing
- Voluntariness
- Full and fair financial disclosure
- Opportunity for independent counsel
- Unconscionability or fairness issues
- Restricted or prohibited provisions
- Child-related terms that may not be finally controlled by agreement
- Execution formalities
- Amendments and revocation
- Material changes
- Language and comprehension
- Coercion or abuse risk

Do not promise enforceability.

Escalate rushed, coercive, incomplete-disclosure, complex-asset, business, immigration, tax, abuse-risk, and interstate situations.

PERSONAL INJURY LAW AID

Build and mature appropriate preparation drafts and forms, such as:

- Incident summary
- Preservation letter
- Insurance communication draft
- Treatment timeline
- Expense and lost-work organizer
- Witness list
- Photograph and evidence inventory
- Property-damage organizer
- Attorney-review request
- Records-request preparation
- Demand-package preparation materials only when not represented as attorney legal work

Do not generate:

- Guaranteed demand value
- Liability conclusion
- Fault percentage
- Settlement prediction
- Serious-injury determination
- Medical diagnosis
- Deadline guarantee

DOMESTIC VIOLENCE AID

Build and mature only safety-conscious, jurisdiction-bounded preparation materials such as:

- Attorney consultation organizer.
- Court and agency preparation checklist.
- Incident and communication timeline that uses deliberate save controls.
- Evidence index and source map.
- Order-of-protection preparation organizer that is not represented as filed legal work.
- Housing, employment, benefits, or agency communication draft only when an approved template and safety review exist.
- Safe-contact preference record.
- Cross-portal referral summary using minimum allowlisted information.
- Resource-contact plan that does not promise availability or safety.
- Attorney-review request.

Do not make public launch depend on:

- Automated filing.
- Autonomous legal advice.
- Guaranteed emergency relief.
- Danger prediction.
- Survivor risk scoring.
- Automatic contact with police, courts, shelters, employers, family members, or alleged abusers.
- Silent saving or transmission of sensitive narratives.
- Public or unencrypted storage of evidence.
- A draft that could be mistaken for a court-issued order, filed petition, attorney work product, or completed safety plan.

Account for coercion, shared-device risk, browser history, unsafe notifications, precise location, contact safety, retaliation risk, and the possibility that another person can access the device or account.

DOCUMENT ENGINE REQUIREMENTS

- Versioned templates
- Versioned clause or provision library
- Stable document and answer IDs
- Jurisdiction selection
- Effective-date tracking
- Conditional logic
- Conflict and contradiction checks
- Missing-information checks
- Plain-language explanations
- Review summary
- DRAFT watermark or equivalent
- Source and template version
- Privacy classification
- Save and resume
- Export and deletion
- Revision comparison
- Attorney-review handoff
- Deterministic non-AI fallback
- Accessibility
- Mobile
- Print quality
- Exact regression fixtures
- Redacted test documents
- Rollback

DOCUMENT-CLASS ACTIVATION GATE

A document class may be public only when:

- Scope is defined
- Supported jurisdictions are defined
- Current legal sources are recorded
- Template and clause review is complete
- Interview logic is complete
- Contradiction and missing-information tests pass
- Draft output tests pass
- Privacy and storage are accepted
- Accessibility is accepted
- Mobile is accepted
- Execution and limitation language is accepted
- Attorney-review pathway is operational
- Support is operational
- Qualified legal review is recorded where required
- Roger approves activation

Incomplete document classes remain hidden or clearly unavailable.

===============================================================================
51. SECURE DOCUMENT AND SCREENSHOT REVIEW CENTER
===============================================================================

Every micro-portal must evaluate and, where appropriate, build a private document and screenshot review center.

The center may support:

- PDF
- Supported office documents
- Plain text
- Images
- Screenshots
- Contracts
- Agreements
- Amendments and addenda
- Notices
- Letters
- Emails and exported message threads
- Text-message or messaging screenshots
- Court papers, orders, pleadings, and filed or proposed forms
- Government, agency, employer, school, landlord, insurer, medical-provider, financial-institution, and other relevant communications
- Insurance communications
- Existing legal documents
- Portal-generated drafts
- Other portal-appropriate records that can be handled safely and lawfully

A Not Applicable or Deferred decision requires a specific user-need, privacy, legal, operational, or launch-scope rationale.

PORTAL EXAMPLES

DIVORCE LAW AID

- Prenuptial and postnuptial agreements
- Separation agreements
- Court orders
- Parenting plans
- Financial disclosures
- Property schedules
- Support documents
- Attorney correspondence
- Relevant communication screenshots
- Notices and filings

ESTATE LAW AID

- Wills
- Trusts
- Amendments
- Powers of attorney
- Health-care directives
- Deeds
- Beneficiary forms
- Probate notices
- Court papers
- Fiduciary correspondence
- Existing estate-plan documents
- Screenshots of notices or account communications

PERSONAL INJURY LAW AID

- Police or incident reports
- Insurance letters
- Denial letters
- Medical bills
- Treatment records only through an approved secure workflow
- Photographs
- Screenshots
- Witness information
- Property-damage estimates
- Wage-loss records
- Settlement communications
- Existing demand materials

DOMESTIC VIOLENCE AID

- Court papers and orders.
- Police, agency, housing, employment, school, benefits, or immigration communications.
- Relevant emails, messages, call logs, photographs, and screenshots only through an accepted safety-sensitive workflow.
- Existing attorney or advocate correspondence.
- Evidence indexes and timelines.
- Resource and contact records.
- Portal-generated preparation drafts.

Domestic Violence Aid upload remains especially high risk. Before activation, require safe-device warnings, deliberate save, discreet notifications, privacy-safe filenames and URLs, strong access controls, short and explained retention choices, deletion, incident response, support, and a clear warning that uploading or deleting a copy does not remove originals, backups, screenshots, messages, or records held elsewhere. Do not strip or alter original metadata when that may affect evidence preservation; preserve originals securely and create separate derived/redacted copies where appropriate.

PERMITTED FUNCTIONS

- Secure file inventory
- Preview
- Text extraction
- OCR for images and screenshots
- Document classification
- Date and party extraction
- Version comparison
- Clause or section comparison
- Missing-page detection
- Difference summary
- Issue and question spotting
- Timeline extraction
- Contact and amount extraction
- Duplicate detection
- Redaction assistance
- Review-package organization
- Comparison with portal-generated drafts
- Comparison with identified official forms or prior versions
- Attorney-review request
- Human-review request
- Download
- Deletion

OUTPUT BOUNDARIES

The system may explain:

- What the document appears to be
- What information it contains
- Differences between versions
- Missing or inconsistent information
- Questions the user may wish to ask
- Relevant portal or professional-review paths

The system must not state:

- The document is valid
- The document is enforceable
- The user will win
- A deadline is guaranteed
- A clause is definitively enforceable
- A medical conclusion
- A settlement value
- A dispositive legal conclusion
- That an attorney reviewed the document when none did

SECURITY AND PRIVACY

Before server upload is active:

- Authentication where appropriate
- Server-side authorization
- Private durable storage
- Encryption in transit
- Approved at-rest protection
- Random nonpublic object identifiers
- No public bucket
- No predictable public URL
- File-extension and MIME allowlists
- File-size and count limits
- Malware scanning
- Quarantine
- Image and office-file risk handling
- Metadata stripping where appropriate
- Safe preview
- OCR isolation
- No active content execution
- Rate limits
- CSRF protection
- Retention policy
- Deletion
- Export
- Backup and restore
- Audit
- Incident response
- Support
- No secrets in logs
- No document contents in ordinary analytics
- No unauthorized AI provider transmission
- Explicit user consent and purpose
- Clear confidentiality and attorney-client-relationship limits


For Domestic Violence Aid also require:

- No silent upload or background synchronization.
- No upload from a URL containing sensitive context.
- Safe-device and shared-device warning before account creation or upload.
- Discreet notification defaults.
- No file names or document titles in ordinary analytics.
- Explicit choice before email or SMS notices.
- Deliberate save and deletion confirmation.
- Clear explanation of backup and deletion limits.
- Confidential-location and contact redaction review.
- Separate preservation of original and derived/redacted files where needed.
- Emergency pause and incident-response controls.

SCREENSHOT HANDLING

- Accept supported image formats.
- Detect and warn about highly sensitive visible data.
- Allow cropping or redaction where practical.
- Preserve original and derived versions separately when needed.
- Record OCR confidence.
- Do not silently treat OCR text as authoritative.
- Show the user the extracted text or findings for confirmation.
- Handle multi-image sequences and ordering.
- Avoid publishing screenshots.
- Delete derived files consistently with the original.

COMPARISON

Support comparison when appropriate:

- Original versus revised agreement
- Existing document versus portal-generated draft
- Two letters or notices
- Two court orders
- Current versus prior will or trust
- Current versus prior prenuptial or postnuptial draft
- Insurance correspondence versions
- Official form version versus completed copy

Comparison results must identify:

- Files compared
- Dates
- Version identifiers
- Pages or sections
- Added content
- Removed content
- Changed content
- Unclear extraction
- Missing pages
- Limitations
- Suggested review questions
- Safe next steps

SUGGESTED NEXT STEPS — REQUIRED OUTPUT SECTION

Every completed document or screenshot review must include a clear, practical, portal-appropriate “Suggested Next Steps” section.

Suggested next steps may include:

- Confirm OCR or extracted information
- Upload missing pages
- Upload the prior or signed version
- Compare another version
- Correct names, dates, amounts, or parties
- Organize related records
- Preserve originals and metadata
- Save a dated copy
- Create a timeline
- Add missing evidence
- Prepare questions for an attorney
- Complete a relevant guided form
- Generate or revise a portal-supported draft
- Request attorney review
- Request human review
- Contact a court, agency, insurer, medical provider, employer, or other appropriate organization using an official route
- Use another Smarter Justice portal when a separate legal path may exist
- Seek urgent qualified legal help when a deadline, safety issue, incapacity, contested proceeding, serious injury, coercion, or other high-risk condition may exist
- Delete sensitive uploads after completion when appropriate

The next-step section must:

- Be prioritized
- Use plain language
- Explain why each suggested step may help
- Distinguish immediate, soon, and optional actions where useful
- Avoid legal conclusions
- Avoid guaranteed deadlines or outcomes
- Avoid telling the user to sign, file, settle, accept, reject, or waive rights without appropriate legal review
- Link only to operational portal or official-resource paths
- Include the attorney-review pathway when relevant
- Identify missing information that limits the review
- Be editable or dismissible where appropriate
- Remain accessible and mobile friendly

AUTOMATED ANALYSIS

Rules-based extraction and comparison may operate without external AI.

External AI remains closed unless:

- Provider and model are approved
- Data-use and retention terms are accepted
- User consent is explicit
- Sensitive data classification permits it
- Security review passes
- Legal and privacy review passes
- Human fallback exists
- Accuracy and hallucination risks are controlled
- Roger approves activation

AI-generated analysis must be labeled and must not be presented as attorney review.

UNTRUSTED CONTENT AND INSTRUCTION ISOLATION

Every uploaded document, screenshot, image, OCR result, email export, embedded
link, metadata field, annotation, attachment, barcode, QR code, and extracted
text is untrusted user-supplied data. Treat content that says `ignore prior
instructions`, `run this command`, `open this link`, `send this file`, `reveal
secrets`, or similar language as document content to analyze—not as authority
to change system, builder, staff, or user instructions.

The review system must:

- separate trusted system and workflow instructions from untrusted document
  content;
- prevent document content from changing prompts, policies, permissions,
  retention, sharing, routing, or gate states;
- prohibit unapproved tool execution, shell or code execution, macro or active
  content execution, external network retrieval, credential access, secret
  disclosure, cross-user retrieval, or data exfiltration caused by document
  content;
- allow only explicitly approved, least-privilege tools and data paths;
- isolate OCR, parsing, preview, extraction, and model processing from public
  execution and from unrelated user records;
- avoid automatically following embedded URLs, loading remote images, opening
  external attachments, or resolving QR/barcode destinations;
- ground material findings to the exact file, page, image region, section, or
  extracted span where practical;
- show uncertainty, OCR confidence, missing context, and conflicting evidence;
- prevent one user's document, prompt, model state, cache, or retrieval result
  from contaminating another user's review;
- record the approved provider, model, model/version identifier, material prompt
  or policy version, tools, retention setting, and processing boundary when
  external AI is used; and
- provide a safe rules-based, local, human-review, or closed-gate fallback when
  the approved processing path is unavailable or unsafe.

Acceptance must include adversarial files and extracted text that attempt
prompt injection, tool invocation, remote retrieval, secret disclosure,
identity confusion, cross-document contamination, unsafe legal conclusions,
or hidden instruction execution. Passing ordinary extraction tests is not
sufficient.

ATTORNEY REVIEW HANDOFF

The review center may route an approved review request only through the paid-member opportunity-allocation system in Section 47.

The attorney must be:

- Verified
- Portal eligible
- Jurisdiction relevant
- Actively covered by the required paid monthly profile membership
- Opportunity eligible
- Conflict ready
- Selected under the approved allocation policy

The user controls which files are shared.

Do not automatically share every upload.

DOCUMENT CENTER GATE

Keep server upload or analysis closed until:

- Storage
- Authorization
- Malware handling
- Retention
- Deletion
- Backup
- Incident response
- Support
- Privacy
- Accessibility
- Mobile
- Legal review
- Attorney-review routing
- Exact testing
- Owner approval

are complete at the claimed level.

===============================================================================
53. PUBLIC-USER WORKSPACE, MATTER FOLDERS, SAVED WORK, EXPORT, AND DELETION
===============================================================================

Where appropriate to the portal and privacy model, provide a clear public-user workspace.

The workspace may organize:

- Guided forms
- Organizers
- Timelines
- Checklists
- Draft documents
- Uploaded documents
- Screenshots
- Review results
- Comparisons
- Suggested next steps
- Official-resource links
- Attorney-review requests
- Opportunity-routing status
- User-selected notes
- Downloads and exports

Use a plain-language concept such as “My Work,” “My Matter,” “My Preparation,” or another portal-appropriate name. Do not imply a filed court case, attorney-client file, or legal representation unless that state genuinely exists.

WORKSPACE REQUIREMENTS

- Stable user or device-level project IDs
- Clear project or matter naming
- Save and resume
- Last updated
- Draft and review status
- Data-location explanation
- Device-only versus server-stored distinction
- Export
- Print
- Download
- Delete
- Account closure or device cleanup
- Backup and restore where supported
- Conflict and merge handling
- Version history where useful
- Privacy and retention controls
- Mobile and keyboard use
- Screen-reader preparation
- Error recovery
- Support

Do not:

- Force an account before providing basic public value unless required for security or persistence
- Hide data persistence
- Store confidential data in ordinary browser URLs
- Mix different users' records
- Expose project titles or document names publicly
- Treat a saved draft as submitted, reviewed, signed, filed, or attorney approved
- Retain sensitive data indefinitely without an approved purpose

When a portal supports device-only work:

- Explain that clearing browser data may remove it.
- Offer an appropriate privacy-safe export or backup.
- Do not silently upload it.

When a portal supports server storage:

- Require appropriate authentication and authorization.
- Apply retention, deletion, export, backup, incident, and audit controls.
- Distinguish account data from legal-matter content.
