54. SECURE STATUS, COMMUNICATIONS, NOTIFICATIONS, AND RECOVERY
===============================================================================

Users and professionals need reliable status without false completion claims.

Potential statuses include:

- Draft
- Saved
- Upload received
- Quarantined
- Processing
- OCR confirmation required
- Review ready
- Missing information
- Comparison ready
- Suggested next steps ready
- Attorney review requested
- Consent pending
- Conflict screening pending
- Eligible attorney pool created
- Opportunity offered
- Attorney accepted preliminary contact
- Attorney declined
- Reassignment in progress
- User withdrew
- Support required
- Closed

Avoid ambiguous labels such as:

- Assigned
- Accepted
- Approved
- Reviewed
- Filed
- Final
- Valid
- Complete

unless the exact meaning is visible and accurate.

COMMUNICATIONS

Use appropriate transactional notifications for:

- Save or account-security events
- Upload receipt
- Quarantine or unsupported file
- Review result availability
- Missing information
- User consent
- Attorney-review request
- Opportunity-routing status
- Attorney preliminary acceptance
- Reassignment
- Withdrawal
- Support update
- Deletion or retention event
- Security incident

Notifications must not contain unnecessary confidential content.

Provide:

- Plain-text email where email is used
- Secure in-product status
- Delivery failure handling
- Bounce handling
- Retry rules
- Support
- Audit
- Preference controls
- Marketing separation
- Accessibility
- Localization readiness where appropriate

A failed notification must not silently change the underlying workflow state.

===============================================================================
55. CONFLICT SCREENING, ATTORNEY ACCEPTANCE, CONSENTED FILE SHARING, AND REPRESENTATION BOUNDARY
===============================================================================

Attorney allocation is not the same as representation.

Before detailed confidential content or documents are shared:

1. Confirm the attorney remains eligible.
2. Confirm active paid monthly membership or covered seat.
3. Confirm portal specialty, jurisdiction, and geography.
4. Confirm user consent for the intended disclosure.
5. Share only minimum preliminary information needed for conflict and capacity review.
6. Allow the attorney to decline for conflict, capacity, scope, jurisdiction, or other appropriate reason.
7. Record preliminary acceptance.
8. Let the user control which files and facts are then shared.
9. Preserve withdrawal and revocation controls where practical.
10. State that representation begins only through an appropriate engagement process.

The platform must not:

- Send every upload automatically
- Broadcast confidential material to a large pool
- Expose opposing-party details before the approved conflict step
- Imply attorney review before acceptance
- Treat opening an opportunity as acceptance
- Treat preliminary acceptance as a signed engagement
- Prevent the attorney from conducting independent conflicts review
- Promise confidentiality beyond the actual system and legal relationship

Support:

- Conflict decline
- Capacity decline
- Wrong specialty
- Wrong jurisdiction
- Duplicate request
- Existing counsel
- User withdrawal
- Attorney withdrawal before engagement
- Reassignment
- Limited-recipient routing
- File-level sharing consent
- Download or access audit
- Access expiration
- Revocation
- Support
- Incident response

===============================================================================
56. URGENCY, DEADLINE, SAFETY, AND ESCALATION PATHWAYS
===============================================================================

Domestic Violence Aid must treat safety escalation as a distinct, survivor-centered pathway. It must clearly state that the portal is not an emergency responder and must use only current, source-backed, jurisdiction-appropriate emergency and hotline information. It must not infer danger level from hidden scoring, automatically contact third parties, or expose the user’s search, location, narrative, or safe-contact choice.

Each portal must evaluate whether the user's situation may require urgent professional or official help.

Examples may include:

DIVORCE LAW AID

- Immediate safety concern
- Domestic violence or coercion
- Child-safety concern
- Imminent hearing
- Existing order
- Relocation
- Asset dissipation
- Imminent signing of an agreement

ESTATE LAW AID

- Recent death
- Court citation or probate deadline
- Suspected exploitation
- Incapacity
- Fiduciary misconduct
- Contested will or trust
- Tax or election deadline
- Property or account at risk
- Imminent signing or execution

PERSONAL INJURY LAW AID

- Medical emergency
- Unsafe condition
- Evidence disappearing
- Insurer or opposing-party deadline
- Government-entity involvement
- Hit-and-run or uninsured issue
- Work-related injury with separate Workers' Compensation path
- Possible Medical Malpractice path
- Serious or worsening symptoms

The platform may:

- Encourage emergency services for immediate safety or medical emergencies.
- Link to current official resources.
- Encourage prompt qualified legal review.
- Explain that deadlines may apply.
- Help preserve documents, dates, photographs, and communications.

The platform must not:

- Guarantee a deadline
- Calculate a limitation period as a final legal conclusion
- Tell a user it is safe to wait
- Create panic through unsupported urgency
- Present emergency guidance as individualized legal advice
- Use urgency as a manipulative sales tactic

Every urgency rule must identify:

- Portal
- Trigger
- Jurisdiction sensitivity
- Source
- Review date
- Message
- Escalation path
- Limitations
- Test
- Owner or legal approval where required

===============================================================================
57. ENGLISH-FIRST PLAIN-LANGUAGE AND FUTURE LANGUAGE-ACCESS READINESS
===============================================================================

CURRENT PHASE

English is the only required active interface and content language.

Every material build must improve the English experience where evidence
supports it and preserve the dormant architecture needed for future Spanish
and additional-language activation.

ENGLISH ACCEPTANCE

- Use plain language.
- Define necessary legal terms.
- Avoid internal jargon and unexplained abbreviations.
- Use short, clear instructions.
- Provide examples carefully without implying legal conclusions.
- Keep reading order and form instructions accessible.
- Keep terminology consistent across navigation, tools, forms, drafts,
  document review, profiles, claims, support, and status.
- Ensure errors, validation, consent, privacy, recovery, and safety language is
  complete in English.
- Do not leave untranslated placeholders, translation keys, or mixed-language
  fragments in active public pages.

LANGUAGE-CONTROL READINESS

- Include one accessible, reusable language-control component and stable
  placement.
- Keep English active.
- Keep Spanish and additional-language feature flags off.
- Keep inactive routes out of public navigation, search, sitemaps, canonical
  and alternate-language links, and attorney demonstrations.
- Do not use a clickable dead control.
- Do not make a disabled preview visible without Roger's explicit approval.
- Preserve a safe route, content-key, version, and fallback strategy for later
  activation.
- Verify English remains complete when every alternate-language flag is
  disabled.

DORMANT-ASSET CONTROL

- Preserve reviewed existing translations and bilingual assets where safe.
- Record their version, review state, source alignment, and currentness.
- Prevent accidental rendering or indexing.
- Do not expand dormant translations during ordinary English-first releases.
- Do not allow dormant content to override newer English legal, safety, form,
  or resource truth.
- Record what must be retranslated or reverified before activation.

FUTURE LANGUAGE ACCEPTANCE

When Roger authorizes a Spanish or additional-language phase:

- Translate complete user journeys rather than isolated labels.
- Preserve legal meaning.
- Use qualified review for material legal, privacy, safety, official-form, and
  emergency content.
- Keep source, schema, template, and version alignment.
- Translate errors, validation, consent, privacy, support, claims, forms,
  drafts, upload instructions, review results, suggested next steps, status,
  and recovery.
- Avoid mixed-language failures.
- Provide an obvious active language control.
- Preserve user selection safely.
- Test mobile, keyboard, screen reader, contrast, zoom, reflow, and
  low-bandwidth use.
- Identify machine-assisted content honestly and require qualified human review
  before launch-critical activation.
- Do not activate partial translated journeys as complete public capability.


===============================================================================
57A. PORTAL USE OF THE CENTRAL PROFESSIONAL IDENTITY AND EVIDENCE REGISTRY
===============================================================================

Each portal consumes an exact versioned read-only candidate/currentness packet
from Smarter Justice when central identity evidence is material.

A portal must not:

- independently mint a competing central attorney identity;
- call the OCA registration number a New York bar number;
- create another central account, claimant identity, subscription, seat, or
  entitlement for the same person;
- bulk scrape the individual Attorney Directory when the official bulk dataset
  and central packet are the correct sources;
- automatically publish every OCA row;
- treat a central identity as specialty acceptance;
- copy another portal's profile acceptance;
- write portal-owned specialty conclusions back into central identity truth
  without a receipted proposal and field ownership.

Use a portal-local profile ID and canonical page bound to the central
professional ID and `NY-OCA-` credential. If the exact central packet is
unavailable, the portal may research and prepare candidates, but a New York
profile with a material unresolved central-identity conflict cannot count as an
accepted profile or paid seat.

===============================================================================
57B. PORTAL-SPECIFIC SPECIALTY EVIDENCE AND ACCEPTANCE PACKET
===============================================================================

For each candidate, create a portal-specialty evidence packet containing:

- exact central identity and namespaced credentials;
- current registration observation and individual recheck state;
- portal specialty and jurisdiction;
- current first-party practice evidence;
- relevant court, government, institutional, appointment, accreditation, or
  roster evidence;
- firm and office evidence;
- safe business contact;
- source and review dates;
- evidence limitations;
- duplicate and ambiguity result;
- representation side where relevant;
- language and service claims where supported;
- correction, suppression, removal, and currentness state;
- accept, reject, hold, or revalidate decision.

Use public labels such as `publicly described practice areas` or `reported areas
of practice` when relying on current first-party descriptions. Do not call those
claims independently certified.

One old appearance or decision is not a broad specialty claim. Use several
consistent current items or a current first-party description where appropriate.

===============================================================================
57C. NEW YORK PUBLICATION, CLAIM, OUTREACH, AND OPPORTUNITY RECHECKS
===============================================================================

Require a targeted current OCA Directory recheck before:

- first publication;
- claim approval;
- outreach;
- featured placement;
- opportunity eligibility;
- restoration after a hold;
- resolution of a material discrepancy.

Record exact source, time, match method, observed status, and conflict state.
Respect technical controls and use a hold when a lawful current check cannot be
completed.

The portal's publication receipt must return to central with the exact profile
ID, central ID, credential, portal, source packet, accepted fields, rejected
fields, publication time, currentness window, and suppression/rollback state.

===============================================================================
57D. BUSINESS-CONTACT PRIVACY, GOOD STANDING, AND DISCIPLINE DISPLAY
===============================================================================

Never publish an OCA residential street address. If a source address may be
residential, publish only supported safe business-facing fields and record the
privacy hold.

Display exact registration status and observation date. Do not translate
`currently registered` into `good standing`.

A good-standing label requires a current official certificate or equivalent
accepted evidence. Public discipline requires an exact public official order or
decision, registration-number or high-confidence identity match, and human
review. Do not publish confidential complaints, investigations, informal
outcomes, `no discipline`, `clean record`, or an absence claim.

===============================================================================
57E. COURT AND SPECIALTY EVIDENCE MINIMIZATION
===============================================================================

Portal builders may use lawful official court and specialty sources to support a
carefully scoped practice statement. Record only the minimum attorney-practice
metadata needed:

- attorney;
- role;
- court or authority;
- matter category;
- date;
- source;
- limitation.

Do not retain unnecessary client names, narratives, medical facts, family facts,
immigration facts, financial information, sealed material, confidential records,
or document content.

Do not calculate or publish win rates, settlement averages, verdict values,
`best attorney` rankings, result scores, or inferred success from incomplete
court data.

===============================================================================
57F. EVERY-RELEASE PROFILE-GROWTH PACKET AND RETURN RECEIPTS
===============================================================================

At the beginning of every ordinary material portal release:

1. resolve the newest exact central candidate/currentness packet;
2. record starting profile, firm, relationship, geography, specialty, and
   currentness counts;
3. process recommended new candidates and existing-profile enrichment;
4. perform required individual rechecks;
5. preserve holds, conflicts, and rejected candidates;
6. add a meaningful lawful accepted batch and enrich existing profiles;
7. return exact central receipts;
8. update search, sitemap, pages, sources, correction/removal, currentness,
   profile-growth evidence, Next Version Improvement List, and continuation.

The central packet supports but does not replace independent portal specialty
review. A thin row or unresolved identity does not count. The portal builder
must also discover lawful qualified candidates when the packet does not exhaust
available high-yield sources, then return those candidate proposals to central
for identity resolution.

===============================================================================
57G. STATUS CHANGE, CORRECTION, SUPPRESSION, AND STALE-RESTORATION RESPONSE
===============================================================================

When central sends a status, privacy, correction, suppression, removal, or
conflict packet:

- authenticate and validate the packet;
- apply only central-owned fields and required safety holds;
- preserve portal-owned specialty and publication provenance;
- update page, search, sitemap, cache, export, outreach, opportunity, backup,
  and rollback states;
- return an idempotent receipt;
- fail closed on stale, conflicting, replayed, or unauthorized packets;
- prevent an older artifact or backup from restoring suppressed content.

A portal-local correction may prepare a central correction packet but may not
silently overwrite central registration truth.

===============================================================================
57H. PORTAL TESTS AND REQUIRED RELEASE EVIDENCE
===============================================================================

Add tests for:

- central ID and `NY-OCA-` binding;
- no duplicate central identity, claim, subscription, or seat;
- candidate-packet identity and expiry;
- individual recheck state;
- specialty evidence separation;
- court-evidence minimization;
- residential-address suppression;
- no good-standing inference;
- public-discipline exact source and identity review;
- no absence-of-discipline claim;
- status eligibility and holds;
- accept/reject/hold receipts;
- correction/suppression propagation;
- stale-restoration prevention;
- no outcome ranking;
- profile-growth counts and utility floor;
- portal-specific search, sitemap, accessibility, privacy, public language, and
  rollback.

Required release records include central packet receipt, portal specialty
acceptance packet, individual rechecks, source-currentness report, privacy
review, discipline/good-standing state, growth/enrichment results, central return
receipts, suppression reconciliation, D0-D5 state, test evidence, rollback, and
continuation.

===============================================================================
58. DOCUMENT PROVENANCE, VERSION HISTORY, SIGNATURE, EXECUTION, AND FINALITY CONTROL
===============================================================================

Every portal-created or uploaded document should preserve enough provenance to prevent confusion.

For portal-generated drafts, record:

- Document ID
- Portal
- Document class
- Jurisdiction
- Template version
- Clause or provision versions
- User-answer revision
- Generated time
- Draft state
- Review state
- Attorney-review state
- Superseded state
- Export format
- Source register
- Limitation language

For uploaded documents, record privately as appropriate:

- File ID
- Original filename
- Safe display name
- Upload time
- File type
- Size
- Digest
- Malware or quarantine state
- OCR state
- User-confirmed extraction state
- Comparison group
- Review state
- Retention state
- Deletion state

FINALITY STATES MUST REMAIN DISTINCT

- Draft
- Revised draft
- User reviewed
- Automated checks complete
- Human reviewed
- Attorney reviewed
- Approved for signature
- Signed
- Witnessed
- Notarized
- Filed
- Accepted by court or agency
- Trust funded
- Asset transferred
- Superseded
- Revoked

Do not allow the interface to imply a later state merely because an earlier state occurred.

E-signature, witnessing, notarization, filing, funding, transfer, and court or agency acceptance remain separate gated capabilities.

===============================================================================
59. FEEDBACK, QUALITY MEASUREMENT, COMPLAINTS, AND CONTINUOUS IMPROVEMENT
===============================================================================

Provide privacy-minimized ways to learn whether public tools and workflows are useful.

Potential feedback points:

- Was the pathway understandable?
- Did the form help organize information?
- Was the document review understandable?
- Were suggested next steps useful?
- Was search useful?
- Was the profile accurate?
- Did the claim process work?
- Was attorney-review routing understandable?
- Did the user encounter an error?
- Is accessibility support needed?

Do not collect unnecessary legal narratives through feedback.

QUALITY SIGNALS MAY INCLUDE

- Completion
- Abandonment
- Error
- Restart
- Save and resume
- Export
- Missing-information rate
- OCR correction rate
- Comparison completion
- Suggested-next-step use
- Search zero-result rate
- Profile correction rate
- Claim completion
- Opportunity delivery
- Conflict decline
- Reassignment
- Complaint
- Opt-out
- Support case
- Accessibility issue

Use these signals to identify improvement opportunities, not to manipulate users or rank attorneys secretly.

Every material release should record:

- Tools evaluated
- Evidence reviewed
- Improvements implemented
- Tools preserved unchanged and why
- Tools hidden or deferred
- New defects
- User or support feedback considered
- Next evaluation priority

===============================================================================
60. ADDITIONAL OWNER, STAFF, ANALYTICS, AND KILL-SWITCH CONTROLS
===============================================================================

Authorized owner or staff controls should include, where applicable:

- Pause public profile publication
- Pause a source class
- Pause an ingestion batch
- Suppress a profile
- Revoke profile control
- Pause outreach
- Enforce do-not-contact
- Pause attorney-review intake
- Pause opportunity allocation
- Pause a professional or firm seat
- Pause document upload
- Pause OCR or automated analysis
- Pause external AI
- Pause a document class
- Pause export or download
- Pause portal import
- Roll back to last-known-good
- Display a truthful maintenance state

Kill switches must:

- Be server enforced
- Be role controlled
- Require reason and audit
- Preserve evidence
- Avoid silent data loss
- Have clear recovery
- Be tested

Owner dashboards and analytics must distinguish:

- Candidate
- Accepted profile
- Public unclaimed
- Claimed
- Verified
- Paid monthly member
- Opportunity eligible
- Opportunity offered
- Preliminary attorney acceptance
- Representation unknown or separately recorded
- Sponsored
- Organic
- Simulated
- Staging
- Production

Never inflate:

- Profile counts
- Paid-member counts
- Referral counts
- Attorney-review counts
- Representation counts
- Successful outcome counts

===============================================================================

Use this prompt repeatedly for every registered owner-approved Smarter Justice legal micro-portal, one dedicated repository and builder chat at a time. Apply the separate initial-launch overlay only to Divorce Law Aid, Estate Law Aid, Personal Injury Law Aid, and Domestic Violence Aid.

At every material build:

- Discover the newest exact completed portal artifact.
- Preserve strict verification for every attorney, firm, organization, or service-profile claimant using the correct authority model for that profile class.
- Treat source-based publication, outreach, claimant verification, and profile control as separate gated processes.
- Discover the newest exact Smarter Justice coordination handoff.
- Preserve all verified working functionality.
- Perform the release-type-appropriate portal-wide scan, deep audit, or full
  milestone audit.
- Proactively originate and assess meaningful improvements across all material facets, without waiting for Roger, then implement the strongest complete coherent set of connected improvements that can be responsibly completed and exact-tested.
- Review relevant accepted Smarter Justice and cross-portal practices; adopt or adapt them when suitable and record a specific reason for material divergence.
- In every ordinary material release, add a meaningful lawful batch of new,
  high-quality, specialty-relevant accepted professional profiles and refresh
  or enrich existing accepted profiles. Both outcomes are mandatory.
- Use a Profile-Growth Exception Receipt only for a narrowly classified non-
  ordinary emergency, rollback, recovery, artifact-integrity correction, or
  genuine no-change outcome. Never use time, competing work, difficulty,
  release size, failure to search, or builder preference as an exception.
- Build exactly one unique, full, canonical individual page for each accepted
  public attorney, tax professional, or other approved individual professional;
  keep firm, practice, and organization pages separate and linked.

  fundamentals plus justified superiority without copying or unsupported public
  comparative claims.
- Provide free optional headshot and practice-logo control for verified
  individual claimants and free optional logo control for authorized entities,
  with complete safe-media, accessibility, moderation, removal, and fallback
  behavior.
- Use `Grow your law practice` for attorney/law-firm entry points and `Grow your
  tax practice` for tax-professional entry points, with truthful non-guarantee
  supporting copy.
- Keep search useful at the new scale.
- Maintain a real paid-monthly-member attorney-review and legal-help opportunity-allocation system when its legal, payment, support, privacy, staging, and owner gates are active.
- Maintain the full tool inventory, perform portal-wide regression/truth scans,
  and deeply audit the tools selected by risk, change impact, demonstration
  importance, staleness, or release scope; improve them only when evidence
  supports a genuine benefit.
- Preserve and improve the coherent Document Help Center with separate, truthful entry points for review, comparison, drafting, official forms, guided preparation, attorney-review preparation, and advanced access where supported.
- Preserve and improve the reusable guided-forms system.
- Build and safely mature supported will, trust, prenuptial, postnuptial, and portal-appropriate draft-document workflows; for Domestic Violence Aid, prioritize safety-conscious organizers and preparation materials and keep risky automated legal documents gated.
- Evaluate and safely improve the private document and screenshot review center for the assigned portal, including useful suggested next steps.
- Keep profile claiming, basic editing, professional headshot/practice-logo control, and entity-logo control free.
- Keep public profile pages on the portal.
- Keep central management, billing, and entitlements in Smarter Justice.
- Preserve one central Smarter Justice account, Stripe/payment, commercial, subscription, firm-seat, and entitlement relationship across applicable portals; do not create duplicate portal subscriptions or independent billing truth.
- Preserve specialty-adapted Matter Path, missing-information, readiness, and explainability where relevant, with a useful non-AI fallback and no unsupported legal conclusion.
- Ask Roger to connect a paid AI provider only after the exact secure preparation packet is complete, and never treat a connected key as live acceptance.
- Keep Nolo and 4LegalLeads optional, future, centrally managed, and separately gated rather than initial-launch dependencies.
- Keep paid placement separate and labeled.
- Keep opportunities fixed-fee and legally gated.
- Keep data provenance, currentness, correction, suppression, and removal real.
- Keep integration read-only and fail closed.
- Keep owner and staff operations practical.
- Keep privacy, security, accessibility, performance, monitoring, support, backup, restore, incident, and rollback requirements.
- Preserve unresolved work.
- Update all current truth.
- Package and test the exact final artifact.
- Do not deploy without Roger's explicit approval.
- When Domestic Violence Aid is assigned, preserve and strengthen The Stop Sign Project as an embedded community initiative.
- Apply the survivor-safety overlay to every Domestic Violence Aid public, profile, upload, workspace, analytics, communication, support, and handoff decision.
- Grow approved organization/service-provider profiles safely, without exposing confidential locations or implying availability, endorsement, or emergency response.
- Keep attorney opportunity allocation limited to verified eligible attorneys or covered firm seats.
- Keep the attorney-outreach demonstration path visually excellent, truthful, mobile-ready, and safe.
- Keep membership-value language qualified and separate from guaranteed outcomes.
- Keep Justice Booth claims evidence-classified and current; mention Justice
  Truck only when a current owner-approved source and the exact portal justify
  the reference.
- Build and launch in English only during the current phase; preserve language-control architecture and dormant language assets while keeping Spanish and additional languages inactive until separately approved.
- Provide a clear QR or short-link continuation path without calling it private or exposing confidential data.

Do not stop profile growth at an artificially small number when safe high-yield batches remain available.

Do not lower quality to inflate counts.

Do not rely on stale version numbers.

Do not ask Roger to identify every candidate or improvement.

Do not make changes merely for activity.

Do not claim anything live without evidence.

The recurring mission is to make every registered portal materially better, visually credible to attorneys and public users, stronger in high-quality professional and applicable public-resource coverage, more accurate, more searchable, easier to claim or correct, safer, more accessible, more operationally mature, more useful through coherent document-help workflows, and more integrated with the current exact Smarter Justice architecture on every responsible release cycle. Material improvement—not change volume—is the measure of success.


The recurring claim and growth rule is:

- Build lawful profiles at scale.
- Publish only profiles that pass the applicable publication gate.
- Label source-based profiles truthfully as unclaimed until control is approved.
- Invite professionals carefully through a separate approved outreach program.
- Verify every claimant using the correct attorney, firm, or organization-authority model.
- Grant only authorized control.
- Preserve free basic rights.
- Keep paid products separate.
- Keep every step auditable, suppressible, reversible, and evidence gated.


The recurring product rule also requires:

- One unique full profile page for every approved attorney and firm and every approved Domestic Violence Aid organization/service profile.
- Verification of every claimant before profile control.
- Active paid monthly profile membership before any attorney-review or legal-help opportunity is delivered.
- A fair, auditable, non-outcome-based assignment system.
- Continual evidence-based public-tool improvement.
- A reusable guided-forms system.
- Safe supported wills, trusts, prenuptial agreements, postnuptial agreements, and portal-appropriate draft workflows.
- Secure document and screenshot review where appropriate.
- Practical suggested next steps in every completed review.
- Strict separation of profile publication, claim verification, paid membership, opportunity eligibility, assignment, attorney acceptance, and representation.

The complete recurring product-capability list now includes:
